--- README.md (Markdown, 252 lines, ~2,660 tokens) ---
   1 | # metera-modkit
   2 | 
   3 | > Professional CLI / TUI / AI-agent for **Chronicles of Meterea** modding.
   4 | 
   5 | `modkit` is the replacement for the old `ModKit.py` GUI. It runs as a
   6 | single `.exe` on Windows, macOS and Linux. It can be used in three
   7 | ways:
   8 | 
   9 | | Mode | How | Who it's for |
  10 | | --- | --- | --- |
  11 | | **TUI workbench** *(default when you launch `modkit`)* | full-screen interactive: mod browser, file tree, JSON editor, schema panel, validation, AI chat. | Modders who want a proper modding IDE without learning a CLI. |
  12 | | **CLI commands** | `modkit new …`, `modkit validate …`, etc. | Scripting and CI. |
  13 | | **AI agent** | `modkit agent "сделай мод с огненным мечом"` or `modkit chat`. | Players who don't want to write JSON by hand. |
  14 | 
  15 | Zero-config: works out of the box. The TUI is the default; the AI
  16 | agent is optional and only activates after `modkit init`.
  17 | 
  18 | ## Highlights
  19 | 
  20 | * **TUI workbench** with three panes (mod list / file tree / tabs),
  21 |   live JSON syntax check, schema hints, quick-create templates, and
  22 |   an embedded AI agent chat.
  23 | * **Quick-create templates** for the most common mod types: empty,
  24 |   item, biome, recipe, class, loot table, total-conversion. Both
  25 |   `modkit new --template biome` and the TUI's *New mod* dialog use
  26 |   the same templates.
  27 | * **Three permission modes** —
  28 |   * `ask` (default) — every write / shell call asks the user;
  29 |   * `auto-edit` — reads and writes are silent, shell still asks;
  30 |   * `yolo` — never asks (use with care).
  31 | * **Three ways to talk to the AI agent**:
  32 |   * `modkit "сделай новый биом"` — single-shot task from the command
  33 |     line;
  34 |   * `modkit chat` — interactive REPL with `/mod`, `/mode`, `/reset`
  35 |     commands;
  36 |   * `modkit run task.md` — execute a task described in a markdown
  37 |     / text file;
  38 |   * TUI tab "AI" — chat right inside the workbench.
  39 | * **Pure stdlib** for the agent loop — no `requests`, no extra HTTP
  40 |   libraries. Textual powers the TUI.
  41 | * **Sandboxed** — every path is `safe_join`-guarded against
  42 |   `..`-traversal. The `read_file` / `write_file` / `edit_file` tools
  43 |   can only touch files inside the active mod.
  44 | 
  45 | ## Install (developer mode)
  46 | 
  47 | ```powershell
  48 | git clone <repo>
  49 | cd metera-modkit
  50 | py -m pip install -e .
  51 | py -m modkit --help
  52 | ```
  53 | 
  54 | ## Build a standalone .exe
  55 | 
  56 | ```powershell
  57 | build.bat
  58 | dist\modkit.exe --version
  59 | ```
  60 | 
  61 | The script installs `pyinstaller` on demand and produces
  62 | `dist\modkit.exe` (~16 MB; includes `textual` for the TUI).
  63 | 
  64 | ## First run
  65 | 
  66 | ```powershell
  67 | modkit                       # opens the TUI workbench
  68 | modkit init                  # interactive setup wizard (provider / model / key)
  69 | ```
  70 | 
  71 | Non-interactive:
  72 | 
  73 | ```powershell
  74 | modkit init --provider openai --api-key sk-... --model gpt-4o-mini
  75 | modkit init --provider anthropic --api-key sk-ant-...
  76 | modkit init --provider gemini --api-key AIza...
  77 | modkit init --provider local --base-url http://localhost:1234/v1
  78 | modkit init --provider dummy --mode yolo     # offline stub for CI / tests
  79 | ```
  80 | 
  81 | ## The TUI workbench
  82 | 
  83 | Just run `modkit` (no args) or `modkit tui`. Layout:
  84 | 
  85 | ```
  86 | ┌─ Header: metera-modkit · <mods root> · <provider> ─────────────────────┐
  87 | │ Mods (sidebar) │ Files (tree)  │ Editor / Schema / Validate / AI │
  88 | │                │               │                                  │
  89 | │                │               │                                  │
  90 | └─ Footer: keybindings ─────────────────────────────────────────────┘
  91 | ```
  92 | 
  93 | ### Keyboard shortcuts
  94 | 
  95 | | Shortcut | Action |
  96 | | --- | --- |
  97 | | `Ctrl+N` | New mod (template picker) |
  98 | | `Ctrl+D` | Duplicate the active mod |
  99 | | `Delete` | Delete the active mod (or the open file) |
 100 | | `Ctrl+S` | Save the open file |
 101 | | `F5` | Validate the active mod |
 102 | | `Ctrl+E` | Export the active mod to `.zip` |
 103 | | `Ctrl+Shift+E` | Open the mod folder in the platform file manager |
 104 | | `Ctrl+L` | Focus the mods list |
 105 | | `Ctrl+T` | Focus the file tree |
 106 | | `Ctrl+R` | Refresh |
 107 | | `F1` | Help overlay |
 108 | | `Ctrl+Q` | Quit |
 109 | 
 110 | ### Right-hand tabs
 111 | 
 112 | * **Editor** — TextArea with live JSON syntax check. Saves with
 113 |   `Ctrl+S`. The current file's extension is auto-detected and the
 114 |   matching tree-sitter language is used if installed.
 115 | * **Schema** — shows the relevant entry from `runtime_manifest.json`
 116 |   for the open file. Updates as you open different files.
 117 | * **Validate** — full mod contract check. Use `F5` to re-run. The
 118 |   result is also written to this tab.
 119 | * **AI** — chat with the LLM agent. The agent has access to the same
 120 |   tool set as the CLI agent and can read / write / validate the
 121 |   active mod.
 122 | 
 123 | ### Quick-create templates
 124 | 
 125 | Available in both the TUI's *New mod* dialog and the CLI flag
 126 | `--template`:
 127 | 
 128 | | Key | What you get |
 129 | | --- | --- |
 130 | | `empty` | `mod.json` + `data/main.js` |
 131 | | `item` | + `data/items.json` with one example item |
 132 | | `biome` | + `data/biomes.json` with one example biome |
 133 | | `recipe` | + `data/economy_recipes.json` |
 134 | | `class` | + `data/classes.json` |
 135 | | `loot` | + `data/loot_tables.json` |
 136 | | `total_conversion` | a TC mod (with `total_conversion: true`) |
 137 | 
 138 | ```powershell
 139 | modkit new my_cool_mod --template biome --name "My Cool Mod"
 140 | ```
 141 | 
 142 | ## Common CLI commands
 143 | 
 144 | ```powershell
 145 | modkit doctor                                # sanity check
 146 | modkit providers                             # list providers
 147 | modkit docs "биомы"                          # search bundled docs
 148 | modkit docs --section recipes                # open a specific section
 149 | modkit list                                  # list installed mods
 150 | modkit new my_cool_mod --name "My Cool Mod" --template biome
 151 | modkit --mod my_cool_mod validate            # validate contract
 152 | modkit --mod my_cool_mod agent "сделай data/items.json с тремя предметами"
 153 | modkit chat                                  # REPL
 154 | modkit run task.md                           # run a task from a file
 155 | modkit tui                                   # launch TUI
 156 | ```
 157 | 
 158 | ### Global flags
 159 | 
 160 | | flag | description |
 161 | | --- | --- |
 162 | | `--provider <id>` | LLM provider (overrides config) |
 163 | | `--model <name>` | Model name |
 164 | | `--api-key <key>` | API key (overrides env / config) |
 165 | | `--base-url <url>` | Base URL for local / custom endpoints |
 166 | | `--mode <ask\|auto-edit\|yolo>` | Permission mode |
 167 | | `--mod <id>` | Active mod id |
 168 | | `--mods-dir <path>` | Override mods root (defaults to game's user-data folder) |
 169 | | `--max-iterations <n>` | Cap on agent tool-loop iterations |
 170 | | `--temperature <f>` | Sampling temperature |
 171 | | `--max-tokens <n>` | Max tokens per reply |
 172 | | `--no-color` | Disable ANSI colours |
 173 | | `--no-shell` | Hide the `shell` tool from the agent |
 174 | | `--json` | Output machine-readable JSON from `list` / `new` / `validate` |
 175 | 
 176 | ## Modding contract — short version
 177 | 
 178 | A mod is a folder named `<id>` (lowercase ASCII letters, digits and
 179 | underscores) that lives under the mods root. It must contain a
 180 | `mod.json` and may contain `data/`, `scripts/` and any other folders.
 181 | 
 182 | ```jsonc
 183 | {
 184 |   "id": "my_cool_mod",        // must equal the folder name
 185 |   "name": "My Cool Mod",
 186 |   "version": "1.0.0",
 187 |   "author": "you",
 188 |   "description": "short summary",
 189 |   "dependencies": ["base_game"],
 190 |   "scripts": ["data/main.js"],
 191 |   "data": {
 192 |     "biomes": ["data/biomes.json"],
 193 |     "items":  ["data/items.json"]
 194 |   }
 195 | }
 196 | ```
 197 | 
 198 | The keys in `data` are validated against `runtime_manifest.json`. Each
 199 | entry is the relative path to a JSON file inside the mod folder.
 200 | 
 201 | For the full reference see the bundled `README.md` (search with
 202 | `modkit docs <query>`) or `data/README.md` in the main game repo.
 203 | 
 204 | ## Project layout
 205 | 
 206 | ```
 207 | metera-modkit/
 208 | ├── modkit/                 # the actual library
 209 | │   ├── __main__.py         # entry point
 210 | │   ├── cli.py              # argparse + subcommands
 211 | │   ├── agent.py            # provider-agnostic tool loop
 212 | │   ├── permissions.py      # ask / auto-edit / yolo
 213 | │   ├── validate.py         # mod.json + data + manifest checks
 214 | │   ├── docs.py             # README.md + runtime_manifest indexer
 215 | │   ├── templates.py        # quick-create mod templates
 216 | │   ├── config.py           # ~/.metera-modkit/config.json
 217 | │   ├── paths.py            # frozen-aware resource / user dirs
 218 | │   ├── ui.py               # coloured output + prompts
 219 | │   ├── providers/          # 13 LLM providers (openai, anthropic, …)
 220 | │   ├── tools/              # fs, docs, mod, shell
 221 | │   └── tui/                # Textual TUI app
 222 | ├── resources/              # bundled with the .exe
 223 | │   ├── README.md
 224 | │   ├── runtime_manifest.json
 225 | │   └── mod_template.json
 226 | ├── tests/                  # 23 unit tests
 227 | │   ├── test_smoke.py
 228 | │   └── test_tui.py
 229 | ├── build.spec              # PyInstaller spec
 230 | ├── build.bat               # one-click .exe builder
 231 | └── pyproject.toml
 232 | ```
 233 | 
 234 | ## For the maintainer
 235 | 
 236 | * `runtime_manifest.json`, `README.md` and `mod_template.json` are
 237 |   copied into `resources/` at build time. If the source files in
 238 |   `data/` change, copy them over (or add a small `sync.py`).
 239 | * The default modding contract lives in
 240 |   `modkit/validate.py:validate_mod` and should stay aligned with
 241 |   `runtime_manifest.json`.
 242 | * New LLM providers can be added by appending a `ProviderSpec` to
 243 |   `modkit/providers/registry.py` and implementing a tiny adapter in
 244 |   `modkit/providers/`. All adapters only depend on stdlib `urllib`.
 245 | * New quick-create templates: add an entry to `TEMPLATES` in
 246 |   `modkit/templates.py` — they're picked up automatically by both
 247 |   the CLI and the TUI.
 248 | 
 249 | ## License
 250 | 
 251 | All rights reserved. Chronicles of Meterea © MrKins_XP (GardenXsa).

--- build.bat (Batch, 50 lines, ~279 tokens) ---
   1 | @echo off
   2 | REM Build a standalone modkit.exe via PyInstaller.
   3 | REM Usage: build.bat [--clean]
   4 | REM
   5 | REM Requires Python 3.10+ and `pip install pyinstaller`.
   6 | 
   7 | setlocal
   8 | 
   9 | set "ROOT=%~dp0"
  10 | pushd "%ROOT%"
  11 | 
  12 | where pyinstaller >NUL 2>&1
  13 | if errorlevel 1 (
  14 |     echo [build] pyinstaller not found, installing into the current Python...
  15 |     python -m pip install --upgrade pyinstaller
  16 |     if errorlevel 1 (
  17 |         echo [build] failed to install pyinstaller
  18 |         popd
  19 |         exit /b 1
  20 |     )
  21 | )
  22 | 
  23 | echo [build] installing project dependencies...
  24 | python -m pip install .
  25 | if errorlevel 1 (
  26 |     echo [build] failed to install dependencies
  27 |     popd
  28 |     exit /b 1
  29 | )
  30 | 
  31 | 
  32 | echo [build] removing previous build artefacts...
  33 | if exist build rmdir /s /q build
  34 | if exist dist rmdir /s /q dist
  35 | 
  36 | echo [build] running PyInstaller...
  37 | pyinstaller --noconfirm build.spec
  38 | if errorlevel 1 (
  39 |     echo [build] PyInstaller failed
  40 |     popd
  41 |     exit /b 1
  42 | )
  43 | 
  44 | echo.
  45 | echo [build] done -^> dist\modkit.exe
  46 | echo.
  47 | 
  48 | popd
  49 | endlocal

--- modkit/__main__.py (Python, 45 lines, ~414 tokens) ---
   1 | """Allow `python -m modkit` invocation."""
   2 | 
   3 | import sys
   4 | 
   5 | 
   6 | def _ensure_streams() -> None:
   7 |     """When launched as a GUI-subsystem .exe (console=False) on Windows,
   8 |     ``sys.stdout``/``sys.stderr`` may be ``None``. Replace them with
   9 |     harmless dummy streams so the rest of the codebase can call ``print()``,
  10 |     ``sys.stdout.isatty()``, ``json.dump(..., sys.stdout)`` etc. without
  11 |     crashing. Any real output is still routed to the parent console when
  12 |     the .exe is launched from cmd/PowerShell."""
  13 |     if getattr(sys, "stdout", None) is None or getattr(sys, "stderr", None) is None or getattr(sys, "stdin", None) is None:
  14 |         import io
  15 | 
  16 |         class _Dummy(io.TextIOBase):
  17 |             def write(self, *_args, **_kwargs):
  18 |                 return 0
  19 | 
  20 |             def flush(self):
  21 |                 return None
  22 | 
  23 |             def isatty(self):
  24 |                 return False
  25 |                 
  26 |             def read(self, *_args, **_kwargs):
  27 |                 return ""
  28 |                 
  29 |             def readline(self, *_args, **_kwargs):
  30 |                 return ""
  31 | 
  32 |         if getattr(sys, "stdout", None) is None:
  33 |             sys.stdout = _Dummy()
  34 |         if getattr(sys, "stderr", None) is None:
  35 |             sys.stderr = _Dummy()
  36 |         if getattr(sys, "stdin", None) is None:
  37 |             sys.stdin = _Dummy()
  38 | 
  39 | 
  40 | _ensure_streams()
  41 | from modkit.cli import main  # noqa: E402 - import after stream fixup
  42 | 
  43 | if __name__ == "__main__":
  44 |     raise SystemExit(main())

--- modkit/agent.py (Python, 207 lines, ~1,975 tokens) ---
   1 | """Agent tool-loop. Provider-agnostic.
   2 | 
   3 | The agent receives a user task, calls the LLM with the available tools,
   4 | executes any requested tool calls, feeds the results back, and loops
   5 | until the model returns a turn without tool calls (or we hit the
   6 | iteration cap).
   7 | 
   8 | Also implements a *text-fallback* parser: when a provider doesn't
   9 | emit native tool calls but the model embeds them as JSON in text
  10 | (the format the original ModKit used), we extract and run them too.
  11 | """
  12 | 
  13 | from __future__ import annotations
  14 | 
  15 | import json
  16 | import re
  17 | import uuid
  18 | from dataclasses import dataclass, field
  19 | from typing import Callable, Iterable
  20 | 
  21 | from modkit import docs as docs_index
  22 | from modkit.providers.base import (
  23 |     AssistantTurn,
  24 |     Message,
  25 |     Provider,
  26 |     ProviderError,
  27 |     ToolCall,
  28 | )
  29 | from modkit.tools.registry import ToolContext, ToolRegistry, ToolResult
  30 | 
  31 | 
  32 | JSON_BLOCK_RE = re.compile(r"```(?:json)?\s*([\s\S]+?)```", re.IGNORECASE)
  33 | 
  34 | 
  35 | @dataclass
  36 | class AgentEvent:
  37 |     kind: str  # "assistant_text" | "tool_call" | "tool_result" | "done" | "error"
  38 |     text: str = ""
  39 |     tool_call: ToolCall | None = None
  40 |     tool_result: ToolResult | None = None
  41 |     meta: dict[str, object] = field(default_factory=dict)
  42 | 
  43 | 
  44 | def default_system_prompt() -> str:
  45 |     cheat = docs_index.cheatsheet()
  46 |     return (
  47 |         "You are metera-modkit, a CLI agent that helps people create mods for "
  48 |         "the game 'Chronicles of Meterea' without forcing them to read the full "
  49 |         "modding documentation.\n\n"
  50 |         "Always operate within the user's selected mod directory. Prefer the file "
  51 |         "tools (list_files, read_file, write_file, edit_file) over the shell tool. "
  52 |         "Validate JSON before finishing — call `validate_mod` to confirm.\n\n"
  53 |         "When the user asks about modding concepts, call `docs_search` or "
  54 |         "`schema_lookup` to ground your answer in the actual docs. Do not invent "
  55 |         "field names — look them up.\n\n"
  56 |         "Be concise. Reply in the user's language. After completing changes, "
  57 |         "summarise what you did in 1-3 sentences and stop calling tools.\n\n"
  58 |         f"{cheat}"
  59 |     )
  60 | 
  61 | 
  62 | def run_agent(
  63 |     *,
  64 |     provider: Provider,
  65 |     registry: ToolRegistry,
  66 |     ctx: ToolContext,
  67 |     user_task: str,
  68 |     history: list[Message] | None = None,
  69 |     system: str | None = None,
  70 |     max_iterations: int = 20,
  71 |     on_event: Callable[[AgentEvent], None] | None = None,
  72 | ) -> list[Message]:
  73 |     """Run the agent loop until completion or *max_iterations*.
  74 | 
  75 |     Returns the full message list (without the system prompt) so the
  76 |     caller can persist or continue the conversation.
  77 |     """
  78 |     system_prompt = system if system is not None else default_system_prompt()
  79 |     notify = on_event or (lambda event: None)
  80 |     messages: list[Message] = list(history or [])
  81 |     messages.append(Message(role="user", content=user_task))
  82 | 
  83 |     tools = registry.definitions()
  84 | 
  85 |     for iteration in range(max_iterations):
  86 |         try:
  87 |             turn = provider.chat(messages, tools=tools, system=system_prompt)
  88 |         except (ProviderError, ConnectionError) as exc:
  89 |             notify(AgentEvent(kind="error", text=str(exc)))
  90 |             messages.append(Message(role="assistant", content=f"[error] {exc}"))
  91 |             return messages
  92 | 
  93 |         # Capture native tool calls plus any text-embedded JSON tool calls.
  94 |         native_calls = list(turn.tool_calls)
  95 |         embedded_calls = _extract_text_tool_calls(turn.text) if not native_calls else []
  96 |         all_calls = native_calls + embedded_calls
  97 | 
  98 |         if turn.text:
  99 |             notify(AgentEvent(kind="assistant_text", text=turn.text))
 100 | 
 101 |         # Persist the assistant turn — without embedded-only calls we still
 102 |         # need the text in history for context.
 103 |         messages.append(
 104 |             Message(role="assistant", content=turn.text, tool_calls=native_calls)
 105 |         )
 106 | 
 107 |         if not all_calls:
 108 |             notify(AgentEvent(kind="done", text=turn.text))
 109 |             return messages
 110 | 
 111 |         for call in all_calls:
 112 |             notify(AgentEvent(kind="tool_call", tool_call=call))
 113 |             result = registry.run(call.name, call.arguments, ctx)
 114 |             notify(
 115 |                 AgentEvent(
 116 |                     kind="tool_result",
 117 |                     tool_call=call,
 118 |                     tool_result=result,
 119 |                 )
 120 |             )
 121 |             messages.append(
 122 |                 Message(
 123 |                     role="tool",
 124 |                     content=result.to_json(),
 125 |                     tool_call_id=call.id,
 126 |                     name=call.name,
 127 |                 )
 128 |             )
 129 | 
 130 |     notify(
 131 |         AgentEvent(
 132 |             kind="error",
 133 |             text=f"agent stopped after {max_iterations} iterations without finishing",
 134 |         )
 135 |     )
 136 |     messages.append(
 137 |         Message(
 138 |             role="assistant",
 139 |             content=(
 140 |                 f"[stopped] reached max iterations ({max_iterations}). "
 141 |                 "Re-run with --max-iterations <N> or finish manually."
 142 |             ),
 143 |         )
 144 |     )
 145 |     return messages
 146 | 
 147 | 
 148 | def _extract_text_tool_calls(text: str) -> list[ToolCall]:
 149 |     if not text:
 150 |         return []
 151 |     raw_candidates: list[str] = []
 152 |     for match in JSON_BLOCK_RE.findall(text):
 153 |         raw_candidates.append(match.strip())
 154 |     stripped = text.strip()
 155 |     if stripped.startswith("{") or stripped.startswith("["):
 156 |         raw_candidates.append(stripped)
 157 | 
 158 |     calls: list[ToolCall] = []
 159 |     for raw in raw_candidates:
 160 |         try:
 161 |             payload = json.loads(raw)
 162 |         except json.JSONDecodeError:
 163 |             continue
 164 |         for normalised in _normalise_calls_payload(payload):
 165 |             calls.append(normalised)
 166 |     return calls
 167 | 
 168 | 
 169 | def _normalise_calls_payload(payload: object) -> Iterable[ToolCall]:
 170 |     if isinstance(payload, dict) and isinstance(payload.get("tool_calls"), list):
 171 |         for entry in payload["tool_calls"]:
 172 |             converted = _entry_to_call(entry)
 173 |             if converted is not None:
 174 |                 yield converted
 175 |         return
 176 |     if isinstance(payload, list):
 177 |         for entry in payload:
 178 |             converted = _entry_to_call(entry)
 179 |             if converted is not None:
 180 |                 yield converted
 181 |         return
 182 |     if isinstance(payload, dict):
 183 |         converted = _entry_to_call(payload)
 184 |         if converted is not None:
 185 |             yield converted
 186 | 
 187 | 
 188 | def _entry_to_call(entry: object) -> ToolCall | None:
 189 |     if not isinstance(entry, dict):
 190 |         return None
 191 |     name = entry.get("tool") or entry.get("name")
 192 |     if not name:
 193 |         return None
 194 |     args = entry.get("args") or entry.get("arguments") or {}
 195 |     if isinstance(args, str):
 196 |         try:
 197 |             args = json.loads(args)
 198 |         except json.JSONDecodeError:
 199 |             args = {"_raw": args}
 200 |     if not isinstance(args, dict):
 201 |         args = {"value": args}
 202 |     return ToolCall(
 203 |         id=str(entry.get("id") or f"call_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}"),
 204 |         name=str(name),
 205 |         arguments=args,
 206 |     )

--- modkit/chat_render.py (Python, 60 lines, ~518 tokens) ---
   1 | """Shared presentation-neutral formatting for AI chat events."""
   2 | 
   3 | from __future__ import annotations
   4 | 
   5 | import json
   6 | from dataclasses import dataclass
   7 | 
   8 | from modkit.agent import AgentEvent
   9 | 
  10 | 
  11 | @dataclass(frozen=True)
  12 | class ChatRecord:
  13 |     kind: str
  14 |     title: str
  15 |     body: str = ""
  16 |     is_markdown: bool = False
  17 | 
  18 | 
  19 | def preview_json(value: object, limit: int = 200) -> str:
  20 |     text = json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False)
  21 |     if len(text) <= limit:
  22 |         return text
  23 |     return text[:limit] + "..."
  24 | 
  25 | 
  26 | def preview_text(value: str, limit: int = 300) -> str:
  27 |     if len(value) <= limit:
  28 |         return value
  29 |     return value[:limit] + "..."
  30 | 
  31 | 
  32 | def event_to_record(event: AgentEvent) -> ChatRecord:
  33 |     if event.kind == "assistant_text" and event.text:
  34 |         return ChatRecord(
  35 |             kind="assistant",
  36 |             title="agent",
  37 |             body=event.text,
  38 |             is_markdown=True,
  39 |         )
  40 |     if event.kind == "tool_call" and event.tool_call is not None:
  41 |         return ChatRecord(
  42 |             kind="tool_call",
  43 |             title=f"tool: {event.tool_call.name}",
  44 |             body=preview_json(event.tool_call.arguments),
  45 |         )
  46 |     if event.kind == "tool_result" and event.tool_result is not None:
  47 |         name = event.tool_call.name if event.tool_call is not None else "tool"
  48 |         if event.tool_result.ok:
  49 |             return ChatRecord(kind="tool_result", title=f"ok {name}")
  50 |         return ChatRecord(
  51 |             kind="tool_result",
  52 |             title=f"fail {name}",
  53 |             body=preview_text(event.tool_result.error or ""),
  54 |         )
  55 |     if event.kind == "error":
  56 |         return ChatRecord(kind="error", title="error", body=event.text)
  57 |     if event.kind == "done":
  58 |         return ChatRecord(kind="done", title="done")
  59 |     return ChatRecord(kind=event.kind, title=event.kind, body=event.text)

--- modkit/cli.py (Python, 921 lines, ~9,658 tokens) ---
   1 | """Command-line entry point.
   2 | 
   3 | We expose a single ``modkit`` binary with subcommands. The most
   4 | important ones are:
   5 | 
   6 | * ``modkit init``       вЂ” interactive setup wizard.
   7 | * ``modkit chat``       вЂ” REPL with the agent.
   8 | * ``modkit agent``      вЂ” one-shot task: ``modkit agent "make me a fire sword mod"``.
   9 | * ``modkit run FILE``   вЂ” execute the task described in a markdown / text file.
  10 | * ``modkit new``        вЂ” scaffold a mod folder.
  11 | * ``modkit list``       вЂ” list installed mods.
  12 | * ``modkit validate``   вЂ” run the mod-contract validator.
  13 | * ``modkit docs QUERY`` вЂ” search the bundled docs without calling any LLM.
  14 | * ``modkit providers``  вЂ” list available LLM providers.
  15 | * ``modkit doctor``     вЂ” sanity checks for config, paths, resources.
  16 | 
  17 | All subcommands honour the same global flags for picking the provider,
  18 | permission mode, and mod directory.
  19 | """

  62 | def _force_utf8_io() -> None:
  63 |     """Make sure stdout/stderr can print non-ASCII text on Windows shells.
  64 | 
  65 |     Without this, printing Cyrillic or emoji from README.md crashes with
  66 |     UnicodeEncodeError on Python's default cp1251 / cp866 codec.
  67 |     """
  68 |     for stream_name in ("stdout", "stderr"):
  69 |         stream = getattr(sys, stream_name, None)
  70 |         if stream is None:
  71 |             continue
  72 |         try:
  73 |             stream.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
  74 |         except (AttributeError, ValueError):
  75 |             try:
  76 |                 buffer = getattr(stream, "buffer", None)
  77 |                 if buffer is not None:
  78 |                     setattr(
  79 |                         sys,
  80 |                         stream_name,
  81 |                         io.TextIOWrapper(buffer, encoding="utf-8", errors="replace"),
  82 |                     )
  83 |             except Exception:  # pragma: no cover - best-effort
  84 |                 pass
  85 |

  92 | 
  93 |     Defaults are set to ``argparse.SUPPRESS`` so when this function is used
  94 |     on a subparser through ``parents=[shared]`` an absent flag does NOT
  95 |     overwrite a value already set on the parent namespace.

 245 | def _apply_global_flags(cfg: config_mod.Config, args: argparse.Namespace) -> config_mod.Config:
 246 |     if _g(args, "no_color"):
 247 |         ui.set_color(False)
 248 |     if _g(args, "provider"):
 249 |         cfg.provider = _g(args, "provider")
 250 |     if _g(args, "model"):
 251 |         cfg.model = _g(args, "model")
 252 |     if _g(args, "base_url"):
 253 |         cfg.base_url = _g(args, "base_url")
 254 |     if _g(args, "api_key"):
 255 |         cfg.api_keys[cfg.provider or "_default"] = _g(args, "api_key")
 256 |     if _g(args, "mode"):
 257 |         cfg.permission_mode = _g(args, "mode")
 258 |     if _g(args, "mods_dir"):
 259 |         cfg.mods_dir = _g(args, "mods_dir")
 260 |     if _g(args, "max_iterations"):
 261 |         cfg.max_iterations = _g(args, "max_iterations")
 262 |     if _g(args, "temperature") is not None:
 263 |         cfg.temperature = _g(args, "temperature")
 264 |     if _g(args, "max_tokens") is not None:
 265 |         cfg.max_tokens = _g(args, "max_tokens")
 266 |     return cfg

 269 | def _build_runtime_context(
 270 |     cfg: config_mod.Config,
 271 |     args: argparse.Namespace,
 272 |     *,
 273 |     include_shell: bool = True,
 274 | ) -> tuple[ToolRegistry, ToolContext]:
 275 |     mods_root = resolve_mods_root(cfg.mods_dir or None)
 276 |     mode = Mode.parse(cfg.permission_mode or "ask")
 277 |     mod_root: Path | None = None
 278 |     mod_id = _g(args, "mod") or ""
 279 |     if mod_id:
 280 |         candidate = mods_root / mod_id
 281 |         if not candidate.exists():
 282 |             ui.error(f"--mod '{mod_id}' not found in {mods_root}")
 283 |             raise SystemExit(2)
 284 |         mod_root = candidate
 285 |     no_shell = bool(_g(args, "no_shell"))
 286 |     registry = build_default_registry(include_shell=include_shell and not no_shell)
 287 | 
 288 |     json_output = bool(_g(args, "json_output"))
 289 | 
 290 |     def confirm(name: str, arguments: dict) -> bool:
 291 |         if json_output:
 292 |             return False
 293 |         preview = json.dumps(arguments, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
 294 |         if len(preview) > 800:
 295 |             preview = preview[:800] + "..."
 296 |         ui.warn(f"agent wants to run tool: {ui.bold(name)}")
 297 |         if preview.strip() != "{}":
 298 |             ui.hint(preview)
 299 |         return ui.confirm("СЂР°Р·СЂРµС€РёС‚СЊ?", default=False)
 300 | 
 301 |     ctx = ToolContext(
 302 |         mods_root=mods_root,
 303 |         mod_root=mod_root,
 304 |         mode=mode,
 305 |         confirm=confirm,
 306 |         log=lambda msg: ui.hint(msg),
 307 |         shell_cwd=mod_root or mods_root,
 308 |     )
 309 |     return registry, ctx

 327 |             f"`--api-key`, or the env var ${config_mod.Config._env_for(provider_id)}."
 328 |         )

 343 | def cmd_init(cfg: config_mod.Config, args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
 344 |     """Interactive or non-interactive setup wizard.
 345 | 
 346 |     Non-interactive: pass ``--provider`` (and optionally ``--model``,
 347 |     ``--api-key``, ``--base-url``, ``--mode``) on the command line. The
 348 |     wizard is then skipped and the config is saved immediately. This is
 349 |     the recommended flow for CI / scripted installs.
 350 |     """
 351 |     non_interactive = bool(_g(args, "provider")) or not sys.stdin.isatty()
 352 | 
 353 |     if non_interactive:
 354 |         specs = list_providers()
 355 |         chosen_id = _g(args, "provider") or cfg.provider or "openai"
 356 |         try:
 357 |             chosen = next(s for s in specs if s.id == chosen_id)
 358 |         except StopIteration:
 359 |             ui.error(f"РЅРµРёР·РІРµСЃС‚РЅС‹Р№ РїСЂРѕРІР°Р№РґРµСЂ '{chosen_id}'. Р”РѕРїСѓСЃС‚РёРјС‹Рµ: {', '.join(s.id for s in specs)}")
 360 |             return 2
 361 |         cfg.provider = chosen.id
 362 |         cfg.model = _g(args, "model") or cfg.model or chosen.default_model
 363 |         key = _g(args, "api_key")
 364 |         if key:
 365 |             cfg.api_keys[chosen.id] = key
 366 |         url = _g(args, "base_url")
 367 |         if url:
 368 |             cfg.base_url = url
 369 |         mode = _g(args, "mode")
 370 |         if mode:
 371 |             try:
 372 |                 cfg.permission_mode = Mode(mode).value
 373 |             except ValueError:
 374 |                 ui.error(f"РЅРµРёР·РІРµСЃС‚РЅС‹Р№ СЂРµР¶РёРј '{mode}'. Р”РѕРїСѓСЃС‚РёРјС‹Рµ: {', '.join(m.value for m in Mode)}")
 375 |                 return 2
 376 |         saved_at = config_mod.save(cfg)
 377 |         ui.success(f"РљРѕРЅС„РёРі СЃРѕС…СЂР°РЅС‘РЅ: {saved_at}")
 378 |         ui.hint(f"provider: {cfg.provider}  model: {cfg.model}  mode: {cfg.permission_mode}")
 379 |         ui.hint("Р”Р°Р»СЊС€Рµ: `modkit chat` РёР»Рё `modkit agent \"СЃРґРµР»Р°Р№ РјРѕРґ СЃ РѕРіРЅРµРЅРЅС‹Рј РјРµС‡РѕРј\"`")
 380 |         return 0
 381 | 
 382 |     ui.header("metera-modkit вЂ” РЅР°СЃС‚СЂРѕР№РєР°")
 383 |     specs = list_providers()
 384 |     labels = [f"{s.id:12s}  {s.name}" for s in specs]
 385 |     pick = ui.choose("Р’С‹Р±РµСЂРё РїСЂРѕРІР°Р№РґРµСЂР° LLM:", labels, default=max(0, next((i for i, s in enumerate(specs) if s.id == "openai"), 0)))
 386 |     chosen = specs[pick]
 387 |     cfg.provider = chosen.id
 388 | 
 389 |     model = ui.ask(f"РњРѕРґРµР»СЊ (Enter = {chosen.default_model})", default=chosen.default_model)
 390 |     cfg.model = model or chosen.default_model
 391 | 
 392 |     if chosen.requires_api_key:
 393 |         env_name = config_mod.Config._env_for(chosen.id)
 394 |         existing = cfg.api_key_for(chosen.id)
 395 |         if existing:
 396 |             ui.hint(f"РЅР°Р№РґРµРЅ API РєР»СЋС‡ (РёР· {env_name} РёР»Рё config), РѕСЃС‚Р°РІР»СЋ РєР°Рє РµСЃС‚СЊ. Р’РІРµРґРё РЅРѕРІС‹Р№ РёР»Рё Enter С‡С‚РѕР±С‹ РїСЂРѕРїСѓСЃС‚РёС‚СЊ.")
 397 |         key = ui.ask(f"API РєР»СЋС‡ РґР»СЏ {chosen.name} (Enter = РѕСЃС‚Р°РІРёС‚СЊ РєР°Рє РµСЃС‚СЊ)", default="")
 398 |         if key:
 399 |             cfg.api_keys[chosen.id] = key
 400 |     else:
 401 |         ui.hint(f"{chosen.name} РЅРµ С‚СЂРµР±СѓРµС‚ API РєР»СЋС‡Р°.")
 402 | 
 403 |     if chosen.id in ("local", "custom"):
 404 |         url = ui.ask(f"Base URL (Enter = {chosen.default_base_url or 'РЅРµ СѓРєР°Р·Р°РЅ'})", default=chosen.default_base_url)
 405 |         cfg.base_url = url
 406 | 
 407 |     modes = [f"{m.value:10s} вЂ” {describe_mode(m).split('вЂ”', 1)[1].strip()}" for m in Mode]
 408 |     cur_idx = next((i for i, m in enumerate(Mode) if m.value == cfg.permission_mode), 0)
 409 |     pick_mode = ui.choose("Р РµР¶РёРј СЂР°Р·СЂРµС€РµРЅРёР№:", modes, default=cur_idx)
 410 |     cfg.permission_mode = list(Mode)[pick_mode].value
 411 | 
 412 |     saved_at = config_mod.save(cfg)
 413 |     ui.success(f"РљРѕРЅС„РёРі СЃРѕС…СЂР°РЅС‘РЅ: {saved_at}")
 414 |     ui.hint("Р”Р°Р»СЊС€Рµ: `modkit chat` РёР»Рё `modkit agent \"СЃРґРµР»Р°Р№ РјРѕРґ СЃ РѕРіРЅРµРЅРЅС‹Рј РјРµС‡РѕРј\"`")
 415 |     return 0

 476 | def cmd_list(cfg: config_mod.Config, args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
 477 |     mods_root = resolve_mods_root(cfg.mods_dir or None)
 478 |     if not mods_root.exists():
 479 |         ui.warn(f"РїР°РїРєР° РјРѕРґРѕРІ РїСѓСЃС‚Р°: {mods_root}")
 480 |         return 0
 481 |     mods: list[dict] = []
 482 |     for child in sorted(mods_root.iterdir()):
 483 |         if not child.is_dir():
 484 |             continue
 485 |         meta_path = child / "mod.json"
 486 |         meta: dict = {}
 487 |         if meta_path.exists():
 488 |             try:
 489 |                 meta = json.loads(meta_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
 490 |             except json.JSONDecodeError:
 491 |                 meta = {"_error": "invalid mod.json"}
 492 |         mods.append(
 493 |             {
 494 |                 "folder": child.name,
 495 |                 "id": meta.get("id", ""),
 496 |                 "name": meta.get("name", ""),
 497 |                 "version": meta.get("version", ""),
 498 |                 "author": meta.get("author", ""),
 499 |                 "description": meta.get("description", ""),
 500 |                 "has_mod_json": meta_path.exists(),
 501 |             }
 502 |         )
 503 | 
 504 |     if _g(args, "json_output"):
 505 |         ui.write_json({"mods_root": str(mods_root), "mods": mods})
 506 |         return 0
 507 | 
 508 |     ui.header(f"РњРѕРґС‹ РІ {mods_root}:")
 509 |     if not mods:
 510 |         ui.hint("  (РЅРµС‚ РјРѕРґРѕРІ)")
 511 |         return 0
 512 |     for m in mods:
 513 |         head = f"  {m['folder']:30s}  v{m['version'] or '?':<10s} {m['name']}"
 514 |         ui.hint(head)
 515 |         if args.full and m["description"]:
 516 |             ui.hint(f"      {m['description']}")
 517 |     return 0

 520 | def cmd_new(cfg: config_mod.Config, args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
 521 |     mods_root = resolve_mods_root(cfg.mods_dir or None)
 522 |     registry, ctx = _build_runtime_context(cfg, args, include_shell=False)
 523 |     ctx.mods_root = mods_root
 524 |     mod_id = args.id_flag or args.id
 525 |     if not mod_id:
 526 |         ui.error("РЅСѓР¶РЅРѕ СѓРєР°Р·Р°С‚СЊ id РјРѕРґР°: `modkit new my_mod` РёР»Рё `modkit new --id my_mod`")
 527 |         return 2
 528 |     payload = {
 529 |         "id": mod_id,
 530 |         "name": args.name or mod_id,
 531 |         "author": args.author,
 532 |         "description": args.description,
 533 |     }
 534 |     if args.total_conversion:
 535 |         payload["total_conversion"] = True
 536 |     # We don't need permission prompts for the explicit CLI command, so
 537 |     # we temporarily promote the mode to yolo.
 538 |     original_mode = ctx.mode
 539 |     ctx.mode = Mode.YOLO
 540 |     result = registry.run("new_mod", payload, ctx)
 541 |     ctx.mode = original_mode
 542 |     if not result.ok:
 543 |         if _g(args, "json_output"):
 544 |             ui.write_json(result.to_dict())
 545 |         ui.error(result.error)
 546 |         return 1
 547 |     mod_path = Path(result.data.get("mod_path"))
 548 |     # Apply a quick-create template if requested.
 549 |     template_name = (args.template or "empty").lower()
 550 |     if template_name not in ("empty", "none", ""):
 551 |         from modkit.templates import get_template, write_template
 552 | 
 553 |         fn = get_template(template_name)
 554 |         if fn is None:
 555 |             ui.warn(f"неизвестный шаблон '{template_name}', пропускаю")
 556 |         else:
 557 |             ctx_template = {
 558 |                 "id": mod_id,
 559 |                 "name": args.name or mod_id,
 560 |                 "author": args.author,
 561 |                 "description": args.description,
 562 |             }
 563 |             files = fn(ctx_template)
 564 |             write_template(mod_path, files)
 565 |             # Re-merge mod.json from CLI overrides (template may set its own
 566 |             # values, but CLI flags always win).
 567 |             mj = mod_path / "mod.json"
 568 |             try:
 569 |                 mj_data = json.loads(mj.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
 570 |             except Exception:
 571 |                 mj_data = {}
 572 |             mj_data["id"] = mod_id
 573 |             if args.name:
 574 |                 mj_data["name"] = args.name
 575 |             if args.author:
 576 |                 mj_data["author"] = args.author
 577 |             if args.description:
 578 |                 mj_data["description"] = args.description
 579 |             if args.total_conversion:
 580 |                 mj_data["total_conversion"] = True
 581 |             mj.write_text(
 582 |                 json.dumps(mj_data, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2),
 583 |                 encoding="utf-8",
 584 |             )
 585 |     if _g(args, "json_output"):
 586 |         ui.write_json(result.to_dict())
 587 |         return 0
 588 |     ui.success(f"РјРѕРґ '{mod_id}' СЃРѕР·РґР°РЅ: {mod_path}")
 589 |     for f in sorted(result.data.get("files", [])):
 590 |         ui.hint(f"  + {f}")
 591 |     return 0

 594 | def cmd_validate(cfg: config_mod.Config, args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
 595 |     mods_root = resolve_mods_root(cfg.mods_dir or None)
 596 |     target = args.mod_id or _g(args, "mod")
 597 |     if not target:
 598 |         ui.error("СѓРєР°Р¶Рё РјРѕРґ: `modkit validate <id>` РёР»Рё С„Р»Р°Рі `--mod <id>`")
 599 |         return 2
 600 |     mod_path = mods_root / target
 601 |     if not mod_path.exists():
 602 |         ui.error(f"РјРѕРґ РЅРµ РЅР°Р№РґРµРЅ: {mod_path}")
 603 |         return 2
 604 |     report = validate_mod(mod_path)
 605 |     if _g(args, "json_output"):
 606 |         ui.write_json(report.to_dict())
 607 |         return 0 if report.ok else 1
 608 |     if report.ok:
 609 |         ui.success(f"РјРѕРґ '{target}' РІР°Р»РёРґРµРЅ")
 610 |     else:
 611 |         ui.error(f"РјРѕРґ '{target}' РЅРµРІР°Р»РёРґРµРЅ")
 612 |     for err in report.errors:
 613 |         ui.error(f"  {err}")
 614 |     for warn in report.warnings:
 615 |         ui.warn(f"  {warn}")
 616 |     for info in report.info:
 617 |         ui.info(f"  {info}")
 618 |     return 0 if report.ok else 1

 621 | def cmd_docs(cfg: config_mod.Config, args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
 622 |     query = " ".join(args.query)
 623 |     results = docs_index.search(query, limit=args.limit)
 624 |     if _g(args, "json_output"):
 625 |         ui.write_json({"query": query, "results": results})
 626 |         return 0
 627 |     if not results:
 628 |         ui.warn("РЅРёС‡РµРіРѕ РЅРµ РЅР°Р№РґРµРЅРѕ")
 629 |         return 1
 630 |     if args.full:
 631 |         top = docs_index.find_section(results[0]["id"])
 632 |         if top is not None:
 633 |             ui.header(f"# {top.title}")
 634 |             print(top.body)
 635 |         return 0
 636 |     ui.header(f"РќР°Р№РґРµРЅРѕ {len(results)} СЃРѕРІРїР°РґРµРЅРёР№ РїРѕ '{query}':")
 637 |     for r in results:
 638 |         ui.hint(f"  [{r['id']}] (score {r['score']}) {r['title']}")
 639 |         if r["snippet"]:
 640 |             snippet = r["snippet"].splitlines()[:3]
 641 |             for line in snippet:
 642 |                 ui.hint(f"      {line[:120]}")
 643 |     return 0

 651 | def cmd_run(cfg: config_mod.Config, args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
 652 |     path = Path(args.file).expanduser().resolve()
 653 |     if not path.exists():
 654 |         ui.error(f"С„Р°Р№Р» РЅРµ РЅР°Р№РґРµРЅ: {path}")
 655 |         return 2
 656 |     task = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
 657 |     return _run_agent_once(cfg, args, task)

 660 | def cmd_chat(cfg: config_mod.Config, args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
 661 |     provider = _build_provider_from_cfg(cfg, args)
 662 |     registry, ctx = _build_runtime_context(cfg, args)
 663 |     ui.header(
 664 |         f"metera-modkit chat ({cfg.provider}/{cfg.model or '?'}, mode={cfg.permission_mode})"
 665 |     )
 666 |     ui.hint("РєРѕРјР°РЅРґС‹: /exit, /mod <id>, /mode <ask|auto-edit|yolo>, /reset, /help")
 667 |     history: list = []
 668 |     if args.initial_task:
 669 |         joined = " ".join(args.initial_task)
 670 |         if joined.strip():
 671 |             history = _agent_turn(provider, registry, ctx, cfg, joined, history)
 672 | 
 673 |     while True:
 674 |         try:
 675 |             line = input(ui.color("cyan", "> ")).strip()
 676 |         except (EOFError, KeyboardInterrupt):
 677 |             print()
 678 |             return 0
 679 |         if not line:
 680 |             continue
 681 |         if line in ("/exit", "/quit", ":q"):
 682 |             return 0
 683 |         if line == "/help":
 684 |             ui.hint("/exit вЂ” РІС‹Р№С‚Рё")
 685 |             ui.hint("/mod <id> вЂ” РїРµСЂРµРєР»СЋС‡РёС‚СЊ Р°РєС‚РёРІРЅС‹Р№ РјРѕРґ")
 686 |             ui.hint("/mode <ask|auto-edit|yolo> вЂ” СЃРјРµРЅРёС‚СЊ СЂРµР¶РёРј СЂР°Р·СЂРµС€РµРЅРёР№")
 687 |             ui.hint("/reset вЂ” РѕС‡РёСЃС‚РёС‚СЊ РёСЃС‚РѕСЂРёСЋ С‡Р°С‚Р°")
 688 |             continue
 689 |         if line.startswith("/mod "):
 690 |             new_mod = line.split(" ", 1)[1].strip()
 691 |             candidate = ctx.mods_root / new_mod
 692 |             if not candidate.exists():
 693 |                 ui.error(f"РјРѕРґ '{new_mod}' РЅРµ РЅР°Р№РґРµРЅ РІ {ctx.mods_root}")
 694 |                 continue
 695 |             ctx.mod_root = candidate
 696 |             ctx.shell_cwd = candidate
 697 |             ui.success(f"Р°РєС‚РёРІРЅС‹Р№ РјРѕРґ: {new_mod}")
 698 |             continue
 699 |         if line.startswith("/mode "):
 700 |             try:
 701 |                 ctx.mode = Mode.parse(line.split(" ", 1)[1].strip())
 702 |                 cfg.permission_mode = ctx.mode.value
 703 |                 ui.success(f"СЂРµР¶РёРј СЂР°Р·СЂРµС€РµРЅРёР№: {ctx.mode.value}")
 704 |             except ValueError as exc:
 705 |                 ui.error(str(exc))
 706 |             continue
 707 |         if line == "/reset":
 708 |             history = []
 709 |             ui.success("РёСЃС‚РѕСЂРёСЏ РѕС‡РёС‰РµРЅР°")
 710 |             continue
 711 |         history = _agent_turn(provider, registry, ctx, cfg, line, history)
 712 |

 714 | # в”Ђв”Ђ agent runner shared by `agent` / `run` / `chat` в”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђ
 715 | 
 716 | 
 717 | def _make_event_handler(cfg: config_mod.Config, json_mode: bool) -> Callable[[AgentEvent], None]:
 718 |     if json_mode:
 719 |         def handler(event: AgentEvent) -> None:
 720 |             payload = {"kind": event.kind}
 721 |             if event.text:
 722 |                 payload["text"] = event.text
 723 |             if event.tool_call is not None:
 724 |                 payload["tool"] = event.tool_call.name
 725 |                 payload["arguments"] = event.tool_call.arguments
 726 |             if event.tool_result is not None:
 727 |                 payload["result"] = event.tool_result.to_dict()
 728 |             print(json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False), flush=True)
 729 |         return handler
 730 | 
 731 |     def handler(event: AgentEvent) -> None:
 732 |         record = event_to_record(event)
 733 |         if event.kind == "assistant_text" and event.text:
 734 |             print(record.body)
 735 |         elif event.kind == "tool_call" and event.tool_call is not None:
 736 |             ui.hint(f"  в†’ {record.title} {record.body}".rstrip())
 737 |         elif event.kind == "tool_result" and event.tool_result is not None:
 738 |             status = "ok" if event.tool_result.ok else "fail"
 739 |             color = "green" if event.tool_result.ok else "red"
 740 |             tool_name = event.tool_call.name if event.tool_call else ""
 741 |             ui.hint(f"    {ui.color(color, status)} {tool_name}")
 742 |             if record.body:
 743 |                 ui.hint(f"      {record.body}")
 744 |         elif event.kind == "error":
 745 |             ui.error(record.body)
 746 | 
 747 |     return handler
 748 | 
 749 | 
 750 | def _run_agent_once(
 751 |     cfg: config_mod.Config,
 752 |     args: argparse.Namespace,
 753 |     task: str,
 754 | ) -> int:
 755 |     provider = _build_provider_from_cfg(cfg, args)
 756 |     registry, ctx = _build_runtime_context(cfg, args)
 757 |     handler = _make_event_handler(cfg, _g(args, "json_output", False))
 758 |     try:
 759 |         run_agent(
 760 |             provider=provider,
 761 |             registry=registry,
 762 |             ctx=ctx,
 763 |             user_task=task,
 764 |             max_iterations=cfg.max_iterations or 20,
 765 |             on_event=handler,
 766 |         )
 767 |     except ProviderError as exc:
 768 |         ui.error(str(exc))
 769 |         return 1
 770 |     return 0
 771 | 
 772 | 
 773 | def _agent_turn(
 774 |     provider: Provider,
 775 |     registry: ToolRegistry,
 776 |     ctx: ToolContext,
 777 |     cfg: config_mod.Config,
 778 |     task: str,
 779 |     history: list,
 780 | ) -> list:
 781 |     handler = _make_event_handler(cfg, False)
 782 |     try:
 783 |         return run_agent(
 784 |             provider=provider,
 785 |             registry=registry,
 786 |             ctx=ctx,
 787 |             user_task=task,
 788 |             history=history,
 789 |             max_iterations=cfg.max_iterations or 20,
 790 |             on_event=handler,
 791 |         )
 792 |     except ProviderError as exc:
 793 |         ui.error(str(exc))
 794 |         return history

 800 | def cmd_tui(cfg: config_mod.Config, args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
 801 |     """Launch the terminal-UI workbench."""
 802 |     cfg = _apply_global_flags(cfg, args)
 803 |     
 804 |     try:
 805 |         sys.stdin.fileno()
 806 |         sys.stdout.fileno()
 807 |     except Exception:
 808 |         if sys.platform == "win32":
 809 |             import ctypes
 810 |             ctypes.windll.user32.MessageBoxW(
 811 |                 0,
 812 |                 "Невозможно запустить TUI: отсутствует консоль.",
 813 |                 "metera-modkit",
 814 |                 0x10
 815 |             )
 816 |         else:
 817 |             ui.error("Невозможно запустить TUI: отсутствует консоль.")
 818 |         return 1
 819 | 
 820 |     try:
 821 |         from modkit.tui import launch as launch_tui
 822 |     except ImportError as exc:
 823 |         ui.error(
 824 |             f"для TUI нужен пакет 'textual': {exc}\n"
 825 |             f"поставь: py -m pip install textual"
 826 |         )
 827 |         return 2
 828 |     return launch_tui(cfg)

 831 | def cmd_gui(cfg: config_mod.Config, args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
 832 |     """Launch the native Qt GUI workbench."""
 833 |     cfg = _apply_global_flags(cfg, args)
 834 |     try:
 835 |         from modkit.gui import launch_gui
 836 |     except ImportError as exc:
 837 |         ui.error(
 838 |             f"для GUI нужен пакет 'PySide6': {exc}\n"
 839 |             f"поставь: py -m pip install PySide6"
 840 |         )
 841 |         return 2
 842 |     return launch_gui(cfg)

 857 |     "gui": cmd_gui,
 858 | }

 860 | 
 861 | def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
 862 |     _force_utf8_io()
 863 |     parser = build_parser()
 864 |     # No args: prefer the native Qt GUI. Fall back to the TUI if
 865 |     # PySide6 is missing. Print help if neither is available.
 866 |     if argv is None and len(sys.argv) == 1:
 867 |         try:
 868 |             cfg = config_mod.load()
 869 |         except KeyboardInterrupt:
 870 |             return 130
 871 |         # Try the GUI first.
 872 |         gui_error = ""
 873 |         try:
 874 |             from modkit.gui import launch_gui
 875 |             return launch_gui(cfg)
 876 |         except Exception as e:
 877 |             import traceback
 878 |             gui_error = traceback.format_exc()
 879 | 
 880 |         # Then the TUI.
 881 |         try:
 882 |             from modkit.tui import launch as launch_tui
 883 | 
 884 |             try:
 885 |                 sys.stdin.fileno()
 886 |                 sys.stdout.fileno()
 887 |             except Exception:
 888 |                 if sys.platform == "win32":
 889 |                     import ctypes
 890 |                     msg = f"Не удалось запустить GUI и нет консоли для TUI.\n\nТрейсбек ошибки GUI:\n{gui_error}"
 891 |                     ctypes.windll.user32.MessageBoxW(0, msg, "metera-modkit", 0x10)
 892 |                 return 1
 893 | 
 894 |             return launch_tui(cfg)
 895 |         except ImportError:
 896 |             pass
 897 |         parser.print_help()
 898 |         return 0
 899 |     args = parser.parse_args(argv)
 900 |     if args.command is None:
 901 |         parser.print_help()
 902 |         return 0
 903 |     if args.command == "version":
 904 |         print(__version__)
 905 |         return 0
 906 |     cfg = config_mod.load()
 907 |     cfg = _apply_global_flags(cfg, args)
 908 |     handler = COMMANDS.get(args.command)
 909 |     if handler is None:
 910 |         parser.error(f"unknown command '{args.command}'")
 911 |         return 2
 912 |     try:
 913 |         return handler(cfg, args)
 914 |     except KeyboardInterrupt:
 915 |         ui.warn("прервано пользователем")
 916 |         return 130

 918 |

--- modkit/config.py (Python, 86 lines, ~715 tokens) ---
   1 | """Persistent configuration for metera-modkit.
   2 | 
   3 | Stored at ``<user_config_dir>/config.json``. Keeps last-used provider,
   4 | API keys (one per provider), defaults for permission mode and approval.
   5 | 
   6 | We intentionally do not require this file to exist; ``load()`` returns
   7 | an empty config when nothing has been written yet.
   8 | """
   9 | 
  10 | from __future__ import annotations
  11 | 
  12 | import json
  13 | import os
  14 | from dataclasses import dataclass, field, asdict
  15 | from typing import Any
  16 | 
  17 | from modkit.paths import user_config_dir
  18 | 
  19 | 
  20 | CONFIG_FILE_NAME = "config.json"
  21 | 
  22 | 
  23 | @dataclass
  24 | class Config:
  25 |     """In-memory representation of ``config.json``."""
  26 | 
  27 |     provider: str = ""
  28 |     model: str = ""
  29 |     base_url: str = ""
  30 |     api_keys: dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict)
  31 |     permission_mode: str = "ask"  # ask | auto-edit | yolo
  32 |     temperature: float = 0.4
  33 |     max_tokens: int = 4096
  34 |     max_iterations: int = 20
  35 |     mods_dir: str = ""
  36 | 
  37 |     def api_key_for(self, provider: str) -> str:
  38 |         return self.api_keys.get(provider, "") or os.environ.get(self._env_for(provider), "")
  39 | 
  40 |     @staticmethod
  41 |     def _env_for(provider: str) -> str:
  42 |         return {
  43 |             "openai": "OPENAI_API_KEY",
  44 |             "anthropic": "ANTHROPIC_API_KEY",
  45 |             "gemini": "GEMINI_API_KEY",
  46 |             "openrouter": "OPENROUTER_API_KEY",
  47 |             "deepseek": "DEEPSEEK_API_KEY",
  48 |             "groq": "GROQ_API_KEY",
  49 |             "mistral": "MISTRAL_API_KEY",
  50 |             "together": "TOGETHER_API_KEY",
  51 |             "fireworks": "FIREWORKS_API_KEY",
  52 |             "cohere": "COHERE_API_KEY",
  53 |             "local": "MODKIT_LOCAL_KEY",
  54 |             "custom": "MODKIT_CUSTOM_KEY",
  55 |         }.get(provider, f"{provider.upper()}_API_KEY")
  56 | 
  57 | 
  58 | def config_path() -> str:
  59 |     return os.path.join(user_config_dir(), CONFIG_FILE_NAME)
  60 | 
  61 | 
  62 | def load() -> Config:
  63 |     path = config_path()
  64 |     if not os.path.exists(path):
  65 |         return Config()
  66 |     try:
  67 |         with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
  68 |             data: dict[str, Any] = json.load(f)
  69 |     except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError):
  70 |         return Config()
  71 |     cfg = Config()
  72 |     for key, value in data.items():
  73 |         if hasattr(cfg, key):
  74 |             setattr(cfg, key, value)
  75 |     if not isinstance(cfg.api_keys, dict):
  76 |         cfg.api_keys = {}
  77 |     return cfg
  78 | 
  79 | 
  80 | def save(cfg: Config) -> str:
  81 |     path = config_path()
  82 |     os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(path), exist_ok=True)
  83 |     with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
  84 |         json.dump(asdict(cfg), f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
  85 |     return path

--- modkit/docs.py (Python, 370 lines, ~3,372 tokens) ---
   1 | """Index of Chronicles of Meterea modding docs.
   2 | 
   3 | We parse ``resources/README.md`` once at startup and split it into
   4 | markdown sections. Each section has:
   5 | 
   6 | * ``id`` — slug derived from the heading (e.g. ``6.6-biomes``)
   7 | * ``number`` — section number like ``6.6`` (optional)
   8 | * ``title`` — original heading text
   9 | * ``level`` — heading depth (2 = ``##``, 3 = ``###`` ...)
  10 | * ``body`` — text from the heading up to the next equal-or-shallower one
  11 | 
  12 | We also expose ``resources/runtime_manifest.json`` (parsed) so callers
  13 | have a single place to look up merge policies / required fields.
  14 | 
  15 | The module is consumed by ``modkit.tools.docs`` to expose
  16 | ``docs_search``, ``docs_section`` and ``schema_lookup`` as agent tools.
  17 | """
  18 | 
  19 | from __future__ import annotations
  20 | 
  21 | import json
  22 | import re
  23 | from dataclasses import dataclass, asdict
  24 | from functools import lru_cache
  25 | from pathlib import Path
  26 | from typing import Any, Iterable
  27 | 
  28 | from modkit.paths import resources_dir
  29 | 
  30 | 
  31 | HEADING_RE = re.compile(r"^(#{1,6})\s+(.*?)\s*$")
  32 | NUMBER_RE = re.compile(r"^(\d+(?:\.\d+)*)\.?\s+(.*)$")
  33 | SLUG_RE = re.compile(r"[^a-z0-9]+")
  34 | 
  35 | 
  36 | @dataclass(frozen=True)
  37 | class Section:
  38 |     id: str
  39 |     title: str
  40 |     number: str
  41 |     level: int
  42 |     body: str
  43 | 
  44 |     def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
  45 |         return asdict(self)
  46 | 
  47 | 
  48 | def _slugify(title: str) -> str:
  49 |     raw = title.strip().lower()
  50 |     # Drop emojis / decorative chars by keeping ASCII alnum and basic Cyrillic.
  51 |     raw = re.sub(r"[^\w\s-]", "", raw, flags=re.UNICODE)
  52 |     raw = SLUG_RE.sub("-", raw)
  53 |     return raw.strip("-")
  54 | 
  55 | 
  56 | def _parse_sections(markdown: str) -> list[Section]:
  57 |     lines = markdown.splitlines()
  58 |     headings: list[tuple[int, int, str, str]] = []  # (line_idx, level, number, title)
  59 |     for idx, line in enumerate(lines):
  60 |         m = HEADING_RE.match(line)
  61 |         if not m:
  62 |             continue
  63 |         level = len(m.group(1))
  64 |         raw_title = m.group(2).strip()
  65 |         n_match = NUMBER_RE.match(raw_title)
  66 |         if n_match:
  67 |             number = n_match.group(1)
  68 |             title = n_match.group(2).strip()
  69 |         else:
  70 |             number = ""
  71 |             title = raw_title
  72 |         headings.append((idx, level, number, title))
  73 | 
  74 |     sections: list[Section] = []
  75 |     for i, (start_idx, level, number, title) in enumerate(headings):
  76 |         end_idx = len(lines)
  77 |         for j in range(i + 1, len(headings)):
  78 |             next_level = headings[j][1]
  79 |             if next_level <= level:
  80 |                 end_idx = headings[j][0]
  81 |                 break
  82 |         body = "\n".join(lines[start_idx:end_idx]).rstrip()
  83 |         slug = _slugify(title)
  84 |         section_id = f"{number}-{slug}" if number else slug
  85 |         if not section_id:
  86 |             section_id = f"section-{i}"
  87 |         sections.append(
  88 |             Section(
  89 |                 id=section_id,
  90 |                 title=title,
  91 |                 number=number,
  92 |                 level=level,
  93 |                 body=body,
  94 |             )
  95 |         )
  96 |     return sections
  97 | 
  98 | 
  99 | @lru_cache(maxsize=1)
 100 | def _load_readme() -> str:
 101 |     path = resources_dir() / "README.md"
 102 |     if not path.exists():
 103 |         return ""
 104 |     return path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
 105 | 
 106 | 
 107 | @lru_cache(maxsize=1)
 108 | def all_sections() -> tuple[Section, ...]:
 109 |     return tuple(_parse_sections(_load_readme()))
 110 | 
 111 | 
 112 | @lru_cache(maxsize=1)
 113 | def runtime_manifest() -> dict[str, Any]:
 114 |     path = resources_dir() / "runtime_manifest.json"
 115 |     if not path.exists():
 116 |         return {}
 117 |     try:
 118 |         return json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
 119 |     except json.JSONDecodeError:
 120 |         return {}

 134 |         return {}

 137 |     except json.JSONDecodeError:
 138 |         return {}

 141 | def find_section(query: str) -> Section | None:
 142 |     """Locate a single section by id, number, or partial title match."""
 143 |     if not query:
 144 |         return None
 145 |     q = query.strip().lower()
 146 |     for section in all_sections():
 147 |         if section.id.lower() == q or section.number == query:
 148 |             return section
 149 |     for section in all_sections():
 150 |         if q in section.title.lower():
 151 |             return section
 152 |     for section in all_sections():
 153 |         if q in section.id.lower():
 154 |             return section
 155 |     return None
 156 | 
 157 | 
 158 | def find_sections(query: str) -> list[Section]:
 159 |     """Return all sections whose id / title contains the query, in order."""
 160 |     if not query:
 161 |         return []
 162 |     q = query.strip().lower()
 163 |     out: list[Section] = []
 164 |     for section in all_sections():
 165 |         if q in section.id.lower() or q in section.title.lower():
 166 |             out.append(section)
 167 |     return out
 168 | 
 169 | 
 170 | def search(
 171 |     query: str,
 172 |     *,
 173 |     limit: int = 5,
 174 |     max_snippet_chars: int = 800,
 175 | ) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
 176 |     """Naive token-based search across section titles + bodies.
 177 | 
 178 |     Returns up to ``limit`` matches ordered by score, each with a
 179 |     short snippet so the agent doesn't pay for the entire section
 180 |     body up front. The agent can follow up with ``docs_section`` for
 181 |     full content.
 182 |     """
 183 |     if not query or not query.strip():
 184 |         return []
 185 | 
 186 |     tokens = [t for t in re.split(r"\W+", query.lower(), flags=re.UNICODE) if t]
 187 |     if not tokens:
 188 |         return []
 189 | 
 190 |     scored: list[tuple[float, Section, int]] = []
 191 |     for section in all_sections():
 192 |         title_l = section.title.lower()
 193 |         body_l = section.body.lower()
 194 |         score = 0.0
 195 |         first_hit = -1
 196 |         for token in tokens:
 197 |             in_title = title_l.count(token)
 198 |             in_body = body_l.count(token)
 199 |             if in_title:
 200 |                 score += 5.0 * in_title
 201 |             if in_body:
 202 |                 score += 1.0 * in_body
 203 |             if first_hit < 0:
 204 |                 pos = body_l.find(token)
 205 |                 if pos >= 0:
 206 |                     first_hit = pos
 207 |         if score == 0.0:
 208 |             continue
 209 |         scored.append((score, section, max(first_hit, 0)))
 210 | 
 211 |     scored.sort(key=lambda item: item[0], reverse=True)
 212 |     results: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
 213 |     for score, section, hit_at in scored[:limit]:
 214 |         snippet = _snippet(section.body, hit_at, max_snippet_chars)
 215 |         results.append(
 216 |             {
 217 |                 "id": section.id,
 218 |                 "number": section.number,
 219 |                 "title": section.title,
 220 |                 "level": section.level,
 221 |                 "score": round(score, 2),
 222 |                 "snippet": snippet,
 223 |             }
 224 |         )
 225 |     return results
 226 | 
 227 | 
 228 | def _snippet(body: str, around: int, max_chars: int) -> str:
 229 |     if len(body) <= max_chars:
 230 |         return body
 231 |     half = max_chars // 2
 232 |     start = max(0, around - half)
 233 |     end = min(len(body), start + max_chars)
 234 |     start = max(0, end - max_chars)
 235 |     snippet = body[start:end]
 236 |     if start > 0:
 237 |         snippet = "..." + snippet
 238 |     if end < len(body):
 239 |         snippet = snippet + "..."
 240 |     return snippet
 241 |

 279 |     "visual_assets": "6.31",
 280 |     "visual_asset_packs": "6.31",
 281 |     "scene_visual_rules": "6.31",
 282 |     "ui_runtime": "6.32",
 283 |     "prompt_runtime": "6.32",
 284 |     "gameplay_runtime": "6.32",
 285 |     "electron_runtime": "6.32",
 286 | }

 288 | 
 289 | def schema_lookup(database_key: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
 290 |     """Return metadata for a database key: manifest entry, contract, doc section."""
 291 |     manifest = runtime_manifest()
 292 |     db_files: dict[str, Any] = manifest.get("database_files", {})
 293 |     entry = db_files.get(database_key)
 294 |     contracts: dict[str, Any] = manifest.get("contracts", {})
 295 |     payload: dict[str, Any] = {
 296 |         "key": database_key,
 297 |         "known": entry is not None,
 298 |     }
 299 |     if entry:
 300 |         payload["manifest"] = entry
 301 |     if database_key in contracts:
 302 |         payload["contract"] = contracts[database_key]
 303 | 
 304 |     doc_id = _SCHEMA_HINTS.get(database_key)
 305 |     section: Section | None = None
 306 |     if doc_id:
 307 |         section = find_section(doc_id)
 308 |     if section is None:
 309 |         section = find_section(database_key)
 310 |     if section is not None:
 311 |         payload["doc_section_id"] = section.id
 312 |         payload["doc_section_title"] = section.title
 313 |         payload["doc_body"] = section.body[:4000]
 314 |     return payload

 317 | def cheatsheet() -> str:
 318 |     """Short reference embedded into the agent system prompt.
 319 | 
 320 |     Intentionally compact (~700 tokens) — agents call ``schema_lookup``
 321 |     or ``docs_search`` for the deep details.
 322 |     """
 323 |     manifest = runtime_manifest()
 324 |     db_files: dict[str, Any] = manifest.get("database_files", {})
 325 |     contract = manifest.get("modding_contract", {})
 326 |     required = contract.get("total_conversion", {}).get("required_database_keys", [])
 327 |     merge_policies = contract.get("merge_policies", [])
 328 | 
 329 |     rows: list[str] = []
 330 |     for key in sorted(db_files.keys()):
 331 |         info = db_files[key]
 332 |         policy = info.get("merge_policy", "?")
 333 |         dtype = info.get("default_type", "?")
 334 |         rows.append(f"  {key:24s}  type={dtype:6s}  merge={policy}")
 335 | 
 336 |     return (
 337 |         "Chronicles of Meterea modding — quick reference\n"
 338 |         "================================================\n"
 339 |         "Mod folder layout (lives in the user's mods dir, NOT inside the game folder):\n"
 340 |         "  <mods>/<mod_id>/mod.json          required descriptor\n"
 341 |         "  <mods>/<mod_id>/data/*.json       data sections\n"
 342 |         "  <mods>/<mod_id>/data/main.js      optional JS script\n"
 343 |         "  <mods>/<mod_id>/assets/*          optional assets\n"
 344 |         "\n"
 345 |         "mod.json minimum:\n"
 346 |         "  id          [a-z0-9_]+, matches folder name\n"
 347 |         "  name        human-readable\n"
 348 |         "  version     semver (e.g. 1.0.0)\n"
 349 |         "  author      author name\n"
 350 |         "  description short text\n"
 351 |         "  dependencies [\"base_game\"] usually\n"
 352 |         "  data        { \"<db_key>\": [\"data/<file>.json\"], ... }\n"
 353 |         "\n"
 354 |         f"Merge policies: {', '.join(merge_policies) or 'deepMerge, append, appendUnique, upsertById, replace'}\n"
 355 |         f"Total-Conversion required keys: {', '.join(required) or 'items, eras, classes, races, biomes, world_config, tag_defaults'}\n"
 356 |         "\n"
 357 |         "Database keys (from runtime_manifest.json):\n"
 358 |         + "\n".join(rows)
 359 |         + "\n\n"
 360 |         "Use the tools:\n"
 361 |         "  docs_search   for free-text questions over the docs\n"
 362 |         "  docs_section  to fetch a full section (e.g. '6.6' or 'biomes')\n"
 363 |         "  schema_lookup for the fields/merge-policy of a database key\n"
 364 |     )
 365 |

 367 | def section_dicts() -> Iterable[dict[str, Any]]:
 368 |     for s in all_sections():
 369 |         yield s.to_dict()

 278 |     def _current_api_key(self) -> str:
 279 |         pid = self._cfg.provider or "dummy"
 280 |         return self._cfg.api_keys.get(pid, "")
 281 | 
 282 |     def _on_provider_changed(self, _idx: int) -> None:
 283 |         self._update_provider_info()
 284 | 
 285 |     def _update_provider_info(self) -> None:
 286 |         pid = self.provider_combo.currentData() or "dummy"
 287 |         spec = next((p for p in self._providers if p.id == pid), None)
 288 |         if spec is None:
 289 |             self._env_label.setText("")
 290 |             return
 291 |         env = Config._env_for(spec.id)
 292 |         bits = [f"env: <code>{env}</code>"]
 293 |         if spec.default_model:
 294 |             bits.append(f"модель по умолчанию: <code>{spec.default_model}</code>")
 295 |         if spec.default_base_url:
 296 |             bits.append(f"base URL: <code>{spec.default_base_url}</code>")
 297 |         if not spec.requires_api_key:
 298 |             bits.append("<i>API ключ не обязателен</i>")
 299 |         self._env_label.setText("  ·  ".join(bits))
 300 |         if not self.model_input.currentText().strip() and spec.default_model:
 301 |             self.model_input.setCurrentText(spec.default_model)
 302 |         if not self.base_url_input.text().strip() and spec.default_base_url:
 303 |             self.base_url_input.setText(spec.default_base_url)
 304 |         if not self.api_key_input.text() or self.api_key_input.text() == self._current_api_key():
 305 |             self.api_key_input.setText(self._cfg.api_keys.get(spec.id, ""))
 306 | 
 307 |     def _build_config(self) -> Config:
 308 |         new_cfg = Config(
 309 |             provider=self.provider_combo.currentData() or "dummy",
 310 |             model=self.model_input.currentText().strip(),
 311 |             base_url=self.base_url_input.text().strip(),
 312 |             api_keys=dict(self._cfg.api_keys),
 313 |             permission_mode=self.mode_combo.currentData() or "ask",
 314 |             temperature=float(self.temp_spin.value()),
 315 |             max_tokens=int(self.tokens_spin.value()),
 316 |             max_iterations=int(self.iter_spin.value()),
 317 |             mods_dir=self.mods_dir_input.text().strip(),
 318 |         )
 319 |         key = self.api_key_input.text().strip()
 320 |         if key:
 321 |             new_cfg.api_keys[new_cfg.provider] = key
 322 |         return new_cfg
 323 | 
 324 |     def _on_ping(self) -> None:
 325 |         self.ping_btn.setEnabled(False)
 326 |         self.ping_status.setText("Проверка...")
 327 |         self.ping_status.setStyleSheet("color: #888;")
 328 |         cfg = self._build_config()
 329 |         self._ping_worker = _PingWorker(cfg)
 330 |         self._ping_worker.result.connect(self._on_ping_result)
 331 |         self._ping_worker.start()
 332 | 
 333 |     def _on_ping_result(self, ok: bool, models: list[str], msg: str) -> None:
 334 |         self.ping_btn.setEnabled(True)
 335 |         if ok:
 336 |             self.ping_status.setText("✓ Пинг успешен! Модель поддерживает tools.")
 337 |             self.ping_status.setStyleSheet("color: #5fa55a;")
 338 |             if models:
 339 |                 current = self.model_input.currentText()
 340 |                 self.model_input.clear()
 341 |                 self.model_input.addItems(models)
 342 |                 self.model_input.setCurrentText(current)
 343 |         else:
 344 |             self.ping_status.setText(f"❌ Ошибка: {msg}")
 345 |             self.ping_status.setStyleSheet("color: #cf6679;")
 346 | 
 347 |     def _on_apply(self) -> None:
 348 |         try:
 349 |             new_cfg = self._build_config()
 350 |             save(new_cfg)
 351 |             self.updated_config = new_cfg
 352 |             self._cfg = new_cfg
 353 |         except OSError as exc:
 354 |             QMessageBox.critical(self, "Ошибка", f"Не удалось сохранить: {exc}")
 355 | 
 356 |     def _on_ok(self) -> None:
 357 |         self._on_apply()
 358 |         if self.updated_config is not None:
 359 |             self.accept()
 360 |         else:
 361 |             self.reject()
 362 | 
 363 | 
 364 | def confirm(parent: QWidget | None, prompt: str, *, danger: bool = False) -> bool:
 365 |     """Show a yes / no confirmation dialog. Returns True on yes."""
 366 |     box = QMessageBox(parent)
 367 |     box.setIcon(
 368 |         QMessageBox.Icon.Warning if danger else QMessageBox.Icon.Question
 369 |     )
 370 |     box.setWindowTitle("Подтверждение")
 371 |     box.setText(prompt)
 372 |     yes = box.addButton("Да", QMessageBox.ButtonRole.YesRole)
 373 |     box.addButton("Нет", QMessageBox.ButtonRole.NoRole)
 374 |     box.setDefaultButton(yes if danger else box.addButton("Отмена", QMessageBox.ButtonRole.RejectRole))
 375 |     box.exec()
 376 |     clicked = box.clickedButton()
 377 |     if clicked is None:
 378 |         return False
 379 |     text = clicked.text().lower()
 380 |     return text.startswith("да")

--- modkit/gui/json_highlighter.py (Python, 59 lines, ~638 tokens) ---
   1 | """JSON / JS syntax highlighter for QPlainTextEdit.
   2 | 
   3 | Lightweight implementation that recognises strings, numbers, booleans
   4 | and the most common punctuation. No external dependencies.
   5 | """
   6 | 
   7 | from __future__ import annotations
   8 | 
   9 | from PySide6.QtCore import QRegularExpression
  10 | from PySide6.QtGui import QColor, QSyntaxHighlighter, QTextCharFormat, QFont
  11 | 
  12 | 
  13 | class JsonHighlighter(QSyntaxHighlighter):
  14 |     """Minimal JSON syntax highlighter.
  15 | 
  16 |     Also does a decent job on JavaScript thanks to the shared
  17 |     grammar tokens (string, number, comment for JS).
  18 |     """
  19 | 
  20 |     def __init__(self, parent=None) -> None:
  21 |         super().__init__(parent)
  22 |         self._rules: list[tuple[QRegularExpression, QTextCharFormat]] = []
  23 | 
  24 |         # Strings
  25 |         string_format = QTextCharFormat()
  26 |         string_format.setForeground(QColor("#ce9178"))
  27 |         self._rules.append((QRegularExpression(r'"[^"\\]*(\\.[^"\\]*)*"'), string_format))
  28 | 
  29 |         # Numbers
  30 |         number_format = QTextCharFormat()
  31 |         number_format.setForeground(QColor("#b5cea8"))
  32 |         self._rules.append((QRegularExpression(r"\b-?\d+(\.\d+)?([eE][+-]?\d+)?\b"), number_format))
  33 | 
  34 |         # Booleans / null
  35 |         keyword_format = QTextCharFormat()
  36 |         keyword_format.setForeground(QColor("#569cd6"))
  37 |         keyword_format.setFontWeight(QFont.Weight.Bold)
  38 |         for kw in ("true", "false", "null", "True", "False", "None"):
  39 |             self._rules.append(
  40 |                 (QRegularExpression(rf"\b{kw}\b"), QTextCharFormat(keyword_format))
  41 |             )
  42 | 
  43 |         # Keys (text before colon)
  44 |         key_format = QTextCharFormat()
  45 |         key_format.setForeground(QColor("#9cdcfe"))
  46 |         self._rules.append((QRegularExpression(r'"[^"\\]*(\\.|[^"\\])*"\s*(?=:)'), key_format))
  47 | 
  48 |         # Punctuation
  49 |         punct_format = QTextCharFormat()
  50 |         punct_format.setForeground(QColor("#d4d4d4"))
  51 |         self._rules.append((QRegularExpression(r"[\{\}\[\],:]"), punct_format))
  52 | 
  53 |     def highlightBlock(self, text: str) -> None:  # type: ignore[override]
  54 |         for pattern, fmt in self._rules:
  55 |             it = pattern.globalMatch(text)
  56 |             while it.hasNext():
  57 |                 m = it.next()
  58 |                 self.setFormat(m.capturedStart(), m.capturedLength(), fmt)

  72 | 
  73 | def _open_in_explorer(path: Path) -> None:
  74 |     if platform.system() == "Windows":
  75 |         os.startfile(str(path))  # noqa: S606
  76 |     elif platform.system() == "Darwin":
  77 |         subprocess.Popen(["open", str(path)])
  78 |     else:
  79 |         subprocess.Popen(["xdg-open", str(path)])
  80 | 
  81 | 
  82 | def _export_zip(src: Path, dest: Path) -> None:
  83 |     with zipfile.ZipFile(dest, "w", zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as zf:
  84 |         for root, _, files in os.walk(src):
  85 |             for f in files:
  86 |                 full = Path(root) / f
  87 |                 zf.write(full, full.relative_to(src.parent))

  90 | # ── AI agent thread ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  91 | 
  92 | 
  93 | class _AgentWorker(QThread):
  94 |     """Runs the LLM agent in a background thread and emits messages."""
  95 | 
  96 |     event = Signal(object)  # AgentEvent, kept as object for Qt signal compatibility.
  97 |     error = Signal(str)
  98 |     finished_history = Signal(list)
  99 | 
 100 |     def __init__(self, cfg: config_mod.Config, mod_path: Path | None, mods_root: Path, task: str, history: list) -> None:
 101 |         super().__init__()
 102 |         self.cfg = cfg
 103 |         self.mod_path = mod_path
 104 |         self.mods_root = mods_root
 105 |         self.task = task
 106 |         self.history = history
 107 | 
 108 |     def run(self) -> None:  # type: ignore[override]
 109 |         try:
 110 |             from modkit.agent import run_agent
 111 |             from modkit.providers import build_provider
 112 |             from modkit.tools import build_default_registry
 113 |             from modkit.tools.registry import ToolContext
 114 | 
 115 |             provider = build_provider(
 116 |                 provider_id=self.cfg.provider,
 117 |                 api_key=self.cfg.api_key_for(self.cfg.provider) or "",
 118 |                 model=self.cfg.model or "",
 119 |                 base_url=self.cfg.base_url or "",
 120 |                 temperature=self.cfg.temperature,
 121 |                 max_tokens=self.cfg.max_tokens,
 122 |             )
 123 |             ctx = ToolContext(
 124 |                 mods_root=self.mods_root,
 125 |                 mod_root=self.mod_path,
 126 |                 mode=Mode(self.cfg.permission_mode),
 127 |                 confirm=lambda name, args: True,
 128 |             )
 129 |             registry = build_default_registry(include_shell=False)
 130 |             final_history = run_agent(
 131 |                 provider=provider,
 132 |                 registry=registry,
 133 |                 ctx=ctx,
 134 |                 user_task=self.task,
 135 |                 history=self.history,
 136 |                 max_iterations=self.cfg.max_iterations or 12,
 137 |                 on_event=self.event.emit,
 138 |             )
 139 |             self.finished_history.emit(final_history)
 140 |         except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
 141 |             self.error.emit(str(exc))
 142 | 
 143 | 
 144 | # ── main window ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 145 | 
 146 | 
 147 | class ModKitWindow(QMainWindow):
 148 |     """The main modding workbench window."""

 157 |         self._ai_worker: _AgentWorker | None = None
 158 |         self.chat_history = []

 164 |         central = QWidget()
