 139 | 
 140 | def build_provider(
 141 |     provider_id: str,
 142 |     *,
 143 |     api_key: str = "",
 144 |     model: str = "",
 145 |     base_url: str = "",
 146 |     temperature: float = 0.4,
 147 |     max_tokens: int = 4096,
 148 |     timeout: int = 120,
 149 | ) -> Provider:
 150 |     spec = get_spec(provider_id)
 151 |     chosen_model = model or spec.default_model
 152 |     chosen_url = base_url or spec.default_base_url
 153 |     return spec.cls(
 154 |         api_key=api_key,
 155 |         model=chosen_model,
 156 |         base_url=chosen_url,
 157 |         temperature=temperature,
 158 |         max_tokens=max_tokens,
 159 |         timeout=timeout,
 160 |     )

--- modkit/templates.py (Python, 213 lines, ~1,772 tokens) ---
   1 | """Quick-create mod templates.
   2 | 
   3 | Pure-data templates used by both the TUI and the CLI. Each template
   4 | returns a dict of ``relative_path -> content`` where content may be
   5 | a string, a dict, or a list (the caller is responsible for serialising
   6 | non-string values to JSON).
   7 | 
   8 | The templates only depend on stdlib so they can be imported from
   9 | frozen executables (PyInstaller / cx_Freeze) without pulling in the
  10 | textual TUI runtime.
  11 | """
  12 | 
  13 | from __future__ import annotations
  14 | 
  15 | import json
  16 | from pathlib import Path
  17 | from typing import Any, Callable
  18 | 
  19 | TemplateFn = Callable[[dict[str, Any]], dict[str, Any]]
  20 | 
  21 | 
  22 | def _mod_json(
  23 |     mod_id: str,
  24 |     name: str,
  25 |     *,
  26 |     author: str = "Unknown",
  27 |     description: str = "",
  28 |     total_conversion: bool = False,
  29 |     extra: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
  30 | ) -> dict[str, Any]:
  31 |     base: dict[str, Any] = {
  32 |         "id": mod_id,
  33 |         "name": name,
  34 |         "version": "1.0.0",
  35 |         "author": author,
  36 |         "description": description,
  37 |         "dependencies": ["base_game"],
  38 |         "scripts": ["data/main.js"],
  39 |         "data": {},
  40 |     }
  41 |     if total_conversion:
  42 |         base["total_conversion"] = True
  43 |     if extra:
  44 |         base.update(extra)
  45 |     return base
  46 | 
  47 | 
  48 | def empty_mod(ctx: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
  49 |     return {
  50 |         "mod.json": _mod_json(
  51 |             ctx["id"],
  52 |             ctx.get("name") or ctx["id"],
  53 |             author=ctx.get("author", "Unknown"),
  54 |             description=ctx.get("description", ""),
  55 |         ),
  56 |         "data/main.js": (
  57 |             "// " + ctx["id"] + "\n"
  58 |             "// Entry point for the mod. See docs: `modkit docs scripts`.\n"
  59 |             "module.exports = {};\n"
  60 |         ),
  61 |     }
  62 | 
  63 | 
  64 | def item_mod(ctx: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
  65 |     mod = empty_mod(ctx)
  66 |     mod["data/items.json"] = [
  67 |         {
  68 |             "id": ctx["id"] + "_example_item",
  69 |             "name": "Example Item",
  70 |             "description": "Replace me with your own item.",
  71 |             "stack": 1,
  72 |             "weight": 1.0,
  73 |             "value": 10,
  74 |         }
  75 |     ]
  76 |     mod["mod.json"]["data"]["items"] = ["data/items.json"]
  77 |     return mod
  78 | 
  79 | 
  80 | def biome_mod(ctx: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
  81 |     mod = empty_mod(ctx)
  82 |     mod["data/biomes.json"] = [
  83 |         {
  84 |             "id": ctx["id"] + "_biome",
  85 |             "name": "Example Biome",
  86 |             "description": "Replace me with your own biome.",
  87 |             "ambient_color": "#88ccff",
  88 |         }
  89 |     ]
  90 |     mod["mod.json"]["data"]["biomes"] = ["data/biomes.json"]
  91 |     return mod
  92 | 
  93 | 
  94 | def recipe_mod(ctx: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
  95 |     mod = empty_mod(ctx)
  96 |     mod["data/economy_recipes.json"] = [
  97 |         {
  98 |             "id": ctx["id"] + "_example_recipe",
  99 |             "name": "Example Recipe",
 100 |             "inputs": [{"id": "scrap_metal", "count": 2}],
 101 |             "outputs": [{"id": "metal_ingot", "count": 1}],
 102 |             "station": "workbench",
 103 |         }
 104 |     ]
 105 |     mod["mod.json"]["data"]["economy_recipes"] = ["data/economy_recipes.json"]
 106 |     return mod
 107 | 
 108 | 
 109 | def class_mod(ctx: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
 110 |     mod = empty_mod(ctx)
 111 |     mod["data/classes.json"] = [
 112 |         {
 113 |             "id": ctx["id"] + "_example_class",
 114 |             "name": "Example Class",
 115 |             "description": "Replace me with your own class.",
 116 |             "base_stats": {
 117 |                 "hp": 100,
 118 |                 "stamina": 100,
 119 |                 "strength": 10,
 120 |                 "agility": 10,
 121 |                 "intelligence": 10,
 122 |             },
 123 |         }
 124 |     ]
 125 |     mod["mod.json"]["data"]["classes"] = ["data/classes.json"]
 126 |     return mod
 127 | 
 128 | 
 129 | def loot_table_mod(ctx: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
 130 |     mod = empty_mod(ctx)
 131 |     mod["data/loot_tables.json"] = [
 132 |         {
 133 |             "id": ctx["id"] + "_example_loot",
 134 |             "name": "Example Loot",
 135 |             "rolls": 1,
 136 |             "entries": [
 137 |                 {"id": "scrap_metal", "weight": 80, "count": [1, 2]},
 138 |                 {"id": "metal_ingot", "weight": 15, "count": [1, 1]},
 139 |                 {"id": "rare_gem", "weight": 5, "count": [1, 1]},
 140 |             ],
 141 |         }
 142 |     ]
 143 |     mod["mod.json"]["data"]["loot_tables"] = ["data/loot_tables.json"]
 144 |     return mod
 145 | 
 146 | 
 147 | def total_conversion_mod(ctx: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
 148 |     mod = empty_mod(ctx)
 149 |     mod["mod.json"] = _mod_json(
 150 |         ctx["id"],
 151 |         ctx.get("name") or ctx["id"],
 152 |         author=ctx.get("author", "Unknown"),
 153 |         description=ctx.get("description", ""),
 154 |         total_conversion=True,
 155 |     )
 156 |     return mod
 157 | 
 158 | 
 159 | # Registry: ordered list of (key, label, function).
 160 | TEMPLATES: list[tuple[str, str, TemplateFn]] = [
 161 |     ("empty", "Empty mod (mod.json + main.js)", empty_mod),
 162 |     ("item", "Item mod (items.json example)", item_mod),
 163 |     ("biome", "Biome mod (biomes.json example)", biome_mod),
 164 |     ("recipe", "Recipe mod (economy_recipes.json example)", recipe_mod),
 165 |     ("class", "Class mod (classes.json example)", class_mod),
 166 |     ("loot", "Loot table mod (loot_tables.json example)", loot_table_mod),
 167 |     ("total_conversion", "Total Conversion (TC) mod", total_conversion_mod),
 168 | ]
 169 | 
 170 | 
 171 | def get_template(name: str) -> TemplateFn | None:
 172 |     """Return the template function for ``name`` or None if unknown."""
 173 |     name = (name or "").lower().strip()
 174 |     for key, _, fn in TEMPLATES:
 175 |         if key == name:
 176 |             return fn
 177 |     return None
 178 | 
 179 | 
 180 | def write_template(target: Path, files: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
 181 |     """Write the template's files into ``target`` directory.
 182 | 
 183 |     ``files`` is the dict returned by a template function. Values that
 184 |     are dict / list get serialised to JSON; everything else is written
 185 |     as UTF-8 text.
 186 |     """
 187 |     target = Path(target)
 188 |     for rel, content in files.items():
 189 |         p = target / rel
 190 |         p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
 191 |         if isinstance(content, (dict, list)):
 192 |             p.write_text(
 193 |                 json.dumps(content, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2),
 194 |                 encoding="utf-8",
 195 |             )
 196 |         else:
 197 |             p.write_text(str(content), encoding="utf-8")
 198 | 
 199 | 
 200 | __all__ = [
 201 |     "TEMPLATES",
 202 |     "TemplateFn",
 203 |     "empty_mod",
 204 |     "item_mod",
 205 |     "biome_mod",
 206 |     "recipe_mod",
 207 |     "class_mod",
 208 |     "loot_table_mod",
 209 |     "total_conversion_mod",
 210 |     "get_template",
 211 |     "write_template",
 212 | ]

--- modkit/tools/docs_tools.py (Python, 121 lines, ~1,244 tokens) ---
   1 | """Documentation tools: docs_search / docs_section / schema_lookup.
   2 | 
   3 | These are *the* mechanism we promised the user: instead of dumping
   4 | the README into every system prompt, the agent searches it on demand.
   5 | """
   6 | 
   7 | from __future__ import annotations
   8 | 
   9 | from typing import Any
  10 | 
  11 | from modkit import docs as docs_index
  12 | from modkit.permissions import Kind
  13 | from modkit.tools.registry import Tool, ToolContext, ToolResult
  14 | 
  15 | 
  16 | def _docs_search(args: dict[str, Any], ctx: ToolContext) -> ToolResult:
  17 |     query = str(args.get("query") or "").strip()
  18 |     if not query:
  19 |         return ToolResult(ok=False, error="'query' is required")
  20 |     limit = int(args.get("limit") or 5)
  21 |     limit = max(1, min(limit, 15))
  22 |     results = docs_index.search(query, limit=limit)
  23 |     if not results:
  24 |         return ToolResult(
  25 |             ok=True,
  26 |             content="no matches",
  27 |             data={"results": [], "query": query},
  28 |         )
  29 |     return ToolResult(ok=True, data={"results": results, "query": query})
  30 | 
  31 | 
  32 | def _docs_section(args: dict[str, Any], ctx: ToolContext) -> ToolResult:
  33 |     raw = args.get("id") or args.get("section") or args.get("number") or args.get("title")
  34 |     if not raw:
  35 |         return ToolResult(ok=False, error="provide 'id', 'section', 'number' or 'title'")
  36 |     section = docs_index.find_section(str(raw))
  37 |     if section is None:
  38 |         return ToolResult(ok=False, error=f"section not found: {raw}")
  39 |     max_chars = int(args.get("max_chars") or 8000)
  40 |     body = section.body[:max_chars]
  41 |     return ToolResult(
  42 |         ok=True,
  43 |         content=body,
  44 |         data={
  45 |             "id": section.id,
  46 |             "number": section.number,
  47 |             "title": section.title,
  48 |             "truncated": len(section.body) > max_chars,
  49 |         },
  50 |     )
  51 | 
  52 | 
  53 | def _schema_lookup(args: dict[str, Any], ctx: ToolContext) -> ToolResult:
  54 |     key = str(args.get("database_key") or args.get("key") or "").strip()
  55 |     if not key:
  56 |         return ToolResult(ok=False, error="'database_key' is required")
  57 |     payload = docs_index.schema_lookup(key)
  58 |     return ToolResult(ok=True, data=payload)
  59 | 
  60 | 
  61 | def build_docs_tools() -> list[Tool]:
  62 |     return [
  63 |         Tool(
  64 |             name="docs_search",
  65 |             description=(
  66 |                 "Search the Chronicles of Meterea modding documentation by free-form "
  67 |                 "query. Returns up to 'limit' (default 5) matching sections with title, "
  68 |                 "number and a snippet. Use docs_section afterwards to read a full section."
  69 |             ),
  70 |             parameters={
  71 |                 "type": "object",
  72 |                 "properties": {
  73 |                     "query": {"type": "string"},
  74 |                     "limit": {"type": "integer", "minimum": 1, "maximum": 15},
  75 |                 },
  76 |                 "required": ["query"],
  77 |                 "additionalProperties": False,
  78 |             },
  79 |             kind=Kind.READ,
  80 |             handler=_docs_search,
  81 |         ),
  82 |         Tool(
  83 |             name="docs_section",
  84 |             description=(
  85 |                 "Fetch a full documentation section by id, number, or title fragment. "
  86 |                 "Example ids: '6.6-biomes', numbers like '6.6', or titles like 'Биомы'."
  87 |             ),
  88 |             parameters={
  89 |                 "type": "object",
  90 |                 "properties": {
  91 |                     "id": {"type": "string"},
  92 |                     "section": {"type": "string"},
  93 |                     "number": {"type": "string"},
  94 |                     "title": {"type": "string"},
  95 |                     "max_chars": {"type": "integer", "minimum": 100},
  96 |                 },
  97 |                 "additionalProperties": False,
  98 |             },
  99 |             kind=Kind.READ,
 100 |             handler=_docs_section,
 101 |         ),
 102 |         Tool(
 103 |             name="schema_lookup",
 104 |             description=(
 105 |                 "Look up a database key (e.g. 'items', 'biomes', 'recipes'). "
 106 |                 "Returns its manifest entry (path, merge_policy, default_type), the "
 107 |                 "contract (required fields, if any) and the matching docs section body."
 108 |             ),
 109 |             parameters={
 110 |                 "type": "object",
 111 |                 "properties": {
 112 |                     "database_key": {"type": "string"},
 113 |                 },
 114 |                 "required": ["database_key"],
 115 |                 "additionalProperties": False,
 116 |             },
 117 |             kind=Kind.READ,
 118 |             handler=_schema_lookup,
 119 |         ),
 120 |     ]

--- modkit/tools/fs.py (Python, 286 lines, ~2,792 tokens) ---
   1 | """File-system tools confined to the currently selected mod directory."""
   2 | 
   3 | from __future__ import annotations
   4 | 
   5 | import os
   6 | from pathlib import Path
   7 | from typing import Any
   8 | 
   9 | from modkit.paths import safe_join
  10 | from modkit.permissions import Kind
  11 | from modkit.tools.registry import Tool, ToolContext, ToolResult
  12 | 
  13 | 
  14 | STRING = {"type": "string"}
  15 | NON_NEG_INT = {"type": "integer", "minimum": 0}
  16 | POS_INT = {"type": "integer", "minimum": 1}
  17 | 
  18 | 
  19 | def _require_mod(ctx: ToolContext) -> Path:
  20 |     if ctx.mod_root is None:
  21 |         raise ValueError(
  22 |             "no mod is selected. Create one with `modkit new` or pass --mod <id>"
  23 |         )
  24 |     return ctx.mod_root
  25 | 
  26 | 
  27 | def _list_files(args: dict[str, Any], ctx: ToolContext) -> ToolResult:
  28 |     root = _require_mod(ctx)
  29 |     subdir = args.get("path", "")
  30 |     base = safe_join(root, subdir) if subdir else root
  31 |     if not base.exists():
  32 |         return ToolResult(ok=False, error=f"path not found: {subdir or '.'}")
  33 |     files: list[str] = []
  34 |     if base.is_file():
  35 |         files.append(base.relative_to(root).as_posix())
  36 |     else:
  37 |         for dirpath, _dirnames, filenames in os.walk(base):
  38 |             for name in filenames:
  39 |                 rel = Path(dirpath, name).relative_to(root).as_posix()
  40 |                 files.append(rel)
  41 |     files.sort()
  42 |     return ToolResult(ok=True, data={"files": files, "count": len(files)})
  43 | 
  44 | 
  45 | def _read_file(args: dict[str, Any], ctx: ToolContext) -> ToolResult:
  46 |     root = _require_mod(ctx)
  47 |     path = safe_join(root, args.get("path", ""))
  48 |     if not path.exists():
  49 |         return ToolResult(ok=False, error=f"file not found: {args.get('path')}")
  50 |     if not path.is_file():
  51 |         return ToolResult(ok=False, error=f"not a regular file: {args.get('path')}")
  52 |     max_bytes = int(args.get("max_bytes", 50000))
  53 |     raw = path.read_bytes()
  54 |     truncated = False
  55 |     if len(raw) > max_bytes:
  56 |         raw = raw[:max_bytes]
  57 |         truncated = True
  58 |     text = raw.decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
  59 |     return ToolResult(
  60 |         ok=True,
  61 |         content=text,
  62 |         data={
  63 |             "path": args.get("path"),
  64 |             "bytes": path.stat().st_size,
  65 |             "truncated": truncated,
  66 |         },
  67 |     )
  68 | 
  69 | 
  70 | def _write_file(args: dict[str, Any], ctx: ToolContext) -> ToolResult:
  71 |     root = _require_mod(ctx)
  72 |     path = safe_join(root, args.get("path", ""))
  73 |     path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
  74 |     content = args.get("content", "")
  75 |     if not isinstance(content, str):
  76 |         content = str(content)
  77 |     path.write_text(content, encoding="utf-8")
  78 |     return ToolResult(
  79 |         ok=True,
  80 |         data={"path": args.get("path"), "bytes": path.stat().st_size},
  81 |     )
  82 | 
  83 | 
  84 | def _edit_file(args: dict[str, Any], ctx: ToolContext) -> ToolResult:
  85 |     root = _require_mod(ctx)
  86 |     rel = args.get("path", "")
  87 |     old = args.get("old", "")
  88 |     new = args.get("new", "")
  89 |     replace_all = bool(args.get("replace_all", False))
  90 | 
  91 |     if not isinstance(old, str) or old == "":
  92 |         return ToolResult(ok=False, error="'old' must be a non-empty string")
  93 |     if not isinstance(new, str):
  94 |         return ToolResult(ok=False, error="'new' must be a string")
  95 | 
  96 |     path = safe_join(root, rel)
  97 |     if not path.exists():
  98 |         return ToolResult(ok=False, error=f"file not found: {rel}")
  99 |     text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
 100 |     count = text.count(old)
 101 |     if count == 0:
 102 |         return ToolResult(ok=False, error="'old' substring not found in file")
 103 |     if count > 1 and not replace_all:
 104 |         return ToolResult(
 105 |             ok=False,
 106 |             error=(
 107 |                 f"'old' substring matches {count} places. Pass replace_all=true "
 108 |                 "or provide more context to make it unique."
 109 |             ),
 110 |         )
 111 |     if replace_all:
 112 |         updated = text.replace(old, new)
 113 |     else:
 114 |         updated = text.replace(old, new, 1)
 115 |     path.write_text(updated, encoding="utf-8")
 116 |     return ToolResult(
 117 |         ok=True,
 118 |         data={
 119 |             "path": rel,
 120 |             "replaced": count if replace_all else 1,
 121 |             "bytes": path.stat().st_size,
 122 |         },
 123 |     )
 124 | 
 125 | 
 126 | def _delete_file(args: dict[str, Any], ctx: ToolContext) -> ToolResult:
 127 |     root = _require_mod(ctx)
 128 |     rel = args.get("path", "")
 129 |     path = safe_join(root, rel)
 130 |     if not path.exists():
 131 |         return ToolResult(ok=False, error=f"file not found: {rel}")
 132 |     if path.is_dir():
 133 |         return ToolResult(ok=False, error=f"refusing to delete directory: {rel}")
 134 |     path.unlink()
 135 |     return ToolResult(ok=True, data={"path": rel})
 136 | 
 137 | 
 138 | def _grep(args: dict[str, Any], ctx: ToolContext) -> ToolResult:
 139 |     root = _require_mod(ctx)
 140 |     import re
 141 | 
 142 |     pattern = args.get("pattern", "")
 143 |     if not pattern:
 144 |         return ToolResult(ok=False, error="'pattern' is required")
 145 |     try:
 146 |         regex = re.compile(pattern)
 147 |     except re.error as exc:
 148 |         return ToolResult(ok=False, error=f"invalid regex: {exc}")
 149 |     glob_filter = args.get("path_glob")
 150 |     max_hits = int(args.get("max_hits", 50))
 151 |     matches: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
 152 |     for dirpath, _, filenames in os.walk(root):
 153 |         for name in filenames:
 154 |             file_path = Path(dirpath, name)
 155 |             rel = file_path.relative_to(root).as_posix()
 156 |             if glob_filter and not _path_matches(rel, glob_filter):
 157 |                 continue
 158 |             try:
 159 |                 lines = file_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()
 160 |             except (OSError, UnicodeDecodeError):
 161 |                 continue
 162 |             for line_no, line in enumerate(lines, 1):
 163 |                 if regex.search(line):
 164 |                     matches.append(
 165 |                         {
 166 |                             "path": rel,
 167 |                             "line": line_no,
 168 |                             "text": line[:300],
 169 |                         }
 170 |                     )
 171 |                     if len(matches) >= max_hits:
 172 |                         return ToolResult(
 173 |                             ok=True,
 174 |                             data={
 175 |                                 "matches": matches,
 176 |                                 "truncated": True,
 177 |                                 "count": len(matches),
 178 |                             },
 179 |                         )
 180 |     return ToolResult(
 181 |         ok=True,
 182 |         data={"matches": matches, "truncated": False, "count": len(matches)},
 183 |     )
 184 | 
 185 | 
 186 | def _path_matches(rel: str, glob: str) -> bool:
 187 |     import fnmatch
 188 | 
 189 |     return fnmatch.fnmatch(rel, glob) or fnmatch.fnmatch(os.path.basename(rel), glob)
 190 |

   1 | """Mod-management tools: list, create, validate, select."""
   2 | 
   3 | from __future__ import annotations
   4 | 
   5 | import json
   6 | import re

   9 | 
  10 | from modkit import docs as docs_index

  16 | MOD_ID_RE = re.compile(r"^[a-z0-9_]+$")
  17 | 
  18 | 
  19 | def _list_mods(args: dict[str, Any], ctx: ToolContext) -> ToolResult:
  20 |     if not ctx.mods_root.exists():
  21 |         return ToolResult(ok=True, data={"mods": [], "mods_root": str(ctx.mods_root)})
  22 |     mods: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
  23 |     for child in sorted(ctx.mods_root.iterdir()):
  24 |         if not child.is_dir():
  25 |             continue
  26 |         meta = {}
  27 |         mod_json = child / "mod.json"
  28 |         if mod_json.exists():
  29 |             try:
  30 |                 meta = json.loads(mod_json.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
  31 |             except json.JSONDecodeError:
  32 |                 meta = {"_error": "invalid mod.json"}
  33 |         mods.append(
  34 |             {
  35 |                 "id": meta.get("id") or child.name,
  36 |                 "folder": child.name,
  37 |                 "name": meta.get("name", ""),
  38 |                 "version": meta.get("version", ""),
  39 |                 "has_mod_json": mod_json.exists(),
  40 |                 "is_current": ctx.mod_root is not None
  41 |                 and ctx.mod_root.resolve() == child.resolve(),
  42 |             }
  43 |         )
  44 |     return ToolResult(
  45 |         ok=True,
  46 |         data={
  47 |             "mods": mods,
  48 |             "mods_root": str(ctx.mods_root),
  49 |             "count": len(mods),
  50 |         },
  51 |     )
  52 | 
  53 | 
  54 | def _new_mod(args: dict[str, Any], ctx: ToolContext) -> ToolResult:
  55 |     mod_id = str(args.get("id") or "").strip()
  56 |     if not mod_id:
  57 |         return ToolResult(ok=False, error="'id' is required")
  58 |     if not MOD_ID_RE.match(mod_id):
  59 |         return ToolResult(
  60 |             ok=False,
  61 |             error="'id' must match [a-z0-9_]+ (lowercase ASCII letters, digits, underscores)",
  62 |         )
  63 |     mod_dir = ctx.mods_root / mod_id
  64 |     if mod_dir.exists():
  65 |         return ToolResult(ok=False, error=f"mod folder already exists: {mod_dir}")
  66 | 
  67 |     template = docs_index.mod_template() or {}
  68 |     base_descriptor: dict[str, Any] = dict(template.get("mod_json") or {})
  69 |     base_descriptor["id"] = mod_id
  70 |     base_descriptor["name"] = str(args.get("name") or mod_id.replace("_", " ").title())
  71 |     base_descriptor["author"] = str(args.get("author") or "Unknown")
  72 |     base_descriptor["description"] = str(args.get("description") or "")
  73 |     base_descriptor.setdefault("version", "1.0.0")
  74 |     base_descriptor.setdefault("dependencies", ["base_game"])
  75 |     base_descriptor.setdefault("scripts", [])
  76 |     base_descriptor.setdefault("data", {})
  77 | 
  78 |     if args.get("total_conversion") is True:
  79 |         base_descriptor["total_conversion"] = True
  80 | 
  81 |     mod_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)
  82 |     (mod_dir / "data").mkdir(exist_ok=True)
  83 |     (mod_dir / "mod.json").write_text(
  84 |         json.dumps(base_descriptor, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2),
  85 |         encoding="utf-8",
  86 |     )
  87 | 
  88 |     files_created = ["mod.json", "data/"]
  89 |     template_files = template.get("files") or {}
  90 |     if base_descriptor["scripts"]:
  91 |         for script in base_descriptor["scripts"]:
  92 |             script_path = mod_dir / script
  93 |             script_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
  94 |             content = template_files.get(script)
  95 |             if content is None:
  96 |                 content = "// auto-generated entry point\n"
  97 |             script_path.write_text(content, encoding="utf-8")
  98 |             files_created.append(script)
  99 | 
 100 |     # Auto-select the newly created mod so subsequent tools work on it.
 101 |     ctx.mod_root = mod_dir
 102 | 
 103 |     return ToolResult(
 104 |         ok=True,
 105 |         data={
 106 |             "mod_id": mod_id,
 107 |             "mod_path": str(mod_dir),
 108 |             "files": files_created,
 109 |         },
 110 |     )
 111 | 
 112 | 
 113 | def _validate(args: dict[str, Any], ctx: ToolContext) -> ToolResult:
 114 |     target_id = str(args.get("mod_id") or "").strip()
 115 |     if target_id:
 116 |         mod_path = ctx.mods_root / target_id
 117 |     elif ctx.mod_root is not None:
 118 |         mod_path = ctx.mod_root
 119 |     else:
 120 |         return ToolResult(
 121 |             ok=False,
 122 |             error="no mod selected and no mod_id given. Pass mod_id=... or `modkit --mod <id>`.",
 123 |         )
 124 |     report = run_validation(mod_path)
 125 |     return ToolResult(ok=report.ok, data=report.to_dict())
 126 | 
 127 | 
 128 | def _select_mod(args: dict[str, Any], ctx: ToolContext) -> ToolResult:
 129 |     mod_id = str(args.get("id") or args.get("mod_id") or "").strip()
 130 |     if not mod_id:
 131 |         return ToolResult(ok=False, error="'id' is required")
 132 |     target = ctx.mods_root / mod_id
 133 |     if not target.exists() or not target.is_dir():
 134 |         return ToolResult(ok=False, error=f"mod folder not found: {target}")
 135 |     ctx.mod_root = target
 136 |     return ToolResult(ok=True, data={"mod_id": mod_id, "mod_path": str(target)})

--- modkit/tools/registry.py (Python, 154 lines, ~1,390 tokens) ---
   1 | """Tool registry and dispatch.
   2 | 
   3 | The agent never calls tool handlers directly; everything goes through
   4 | :py:class:`ToolRegistry.run`. That method enforces the permission mode
   5 | (ask / auto-edit / yolo) and serialises results so the model gets
   6 | predictable JSON back.
   7 | 
   8 | Each tool implementation registers itself by returning a :class:`Tool`
   9 | from a builder function. See ``modkit.tools.fs`` etc. for concrete
  10 | examples.
  11 | """
  12 | 
  13 | from __future__ import annotations
  14 | 
  15 | import json
  16 | from dataclasses import dataclass, field
  17 | from pathlib import Path
  18 | from typing import Any, Callable, Iterable, Optional
  19 | 
  20 | from modkit.permissions import Decision, Kind, Mode, evaluate
  21 | from modkit.providers.base import ToolDef
  22 | 
  23 | 
  24 | # A handler receives the parsed arguments and a context, and returns a ToolResult.
  25 | ToolHandler = Callable[[dict[str, Any], "ToolContext"], "ToolResult"]
  26 | 
  27 | 
  28 | @dataclass
  29 | class ToolResult:
  30 |     ok: bool
  31 |     content: str = ""
  32 |     data: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
  33 |     error: str = ""
  34 | 
  35 |     def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
  36 |         payload: dict[str, Any] = {"ok": self.ok}
  37 |         if self.content:
  38 |             payload["content"] = self.content
  39 |         if self.data:
  40 |             payload["data"] = self.data
  41 |         if self.error:
  42 |             payload["error"] = self.error
  43 |         return payload
  44 | 
  45 |     def to_json(self) -> str:
  46 |         return json.dumps(self.to_dict(), ensure_ascii=False)
  47 | 
  48 | 
  49 | @dataclass
  50 | class Tool:
  51 |     name: str
  52 |     description: str
  53 |     parameters: dict[str, Any]
  54 |     kind: Kind
  55 |     handler: ToolHandler
  56 | 
  57 |     def to_def(self) -> ToolDef:
  58 |         return ToolDef(
  59 |             name=self.name, description=self.description, parameters=self.parameters
  60 |         )
  61 | 
  62 | 
  63 | @dataclass
  64 | class ToolContext:
  65 |     """State the tools share across calls (mostly per-task)."""
  66 | 
  67 |     mods_root: Path
  68 |     mod_root: Optional[Path] = None
  69 |     mode: Mode = Mode.ASK
  70 |     confirm: Callable[[str, dict[str, Any]], bool] = lambda name, args: False
  71 |     log: Callable[[str], None] = lambda msg: None
  72 |     shell_cwd: Optional[Path] = None
  73 |     extra: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
  74 | 
  75 | 
  76 | class ToolRegistry:
  77 |     def __init__(self, tools: Iterable[Tool] = ()) -> None:
  78 |         self._tools: dict[str, Tool] = {}
  79 |         for t in tools:
  80 |             self.register(t)
  81 | 
  82 |     def register(self, tool: Tool) -> None:
  83 |         if tool.name in self._tools:
  84 |             raise ValueError(f"tool '{tool.name}' already registered")
  85 |         self._tools[tool.name] = tool
  86 | 
  87 |     def get(self, name: str) -> Optional[Tool]:
  88 |         return self._tools.get(name)
  89 | 
  90 |     def names(self) -> list[str]:
  91 |         return sorted(self._tools)
  92 | 
  93 |     def definitions(self) -> list[ToolDef]:
  94 |         return [t.to_def() for t in self._tools.values()]
  95 | 
  96 |     # ── execution path ────────────────────────────────────────────────
  97 | 
  98 |     def run(
  99 |         self,
 100 |         name: str,
 101 |         arguments: dict[str, Any] | None,
 102 |         ctx: ToolContext,
 103 |     ) -> ToolResult:
 104 |         tool = self._tools.get(name)
 105 |         if tool is None:
 106 |             return ToolResult(ok=False, error=f"unknown tool '{name}'")
 107 |         args = arguments or {}
 108 |         permission = evaluate(ctx.mode, tool.kind)
 109 | 
 110 |         if permission.decision == Decision.DENY:
 111 |             return ToolResult(
 112 |                 ok=False,
 113 |                 error=f"tool '{name}' denied by permission mode '{ctx.mode.value}'",
 114 |             )
 115 |         if permission.decision == Decision.ASK:
 116 |             allowed = ctx.confirm(name, args)
 117 |             if not allowed:
 118 |                 return ToolResult(
 119 |                     ok=False,
 120 |                     error=f"tool '{name}' denied by user",
 121 |                     data={"denied": True},
 122 |                 )
 123 | 
 124 |         try:
 125 |             return tool.handler(args, ctx)
 126 |         except FileNotFoundError as exc:
 127 |             return ToolResult(ok=False, error=f"{exc}")
 128 |         except PermissionError as exc:
 129 |             return ToolResult(ok=False, error=f"permission error: {exc}")
 130 |         except ValueError as exc:
 131 |             return ToolResult(ok=False, error=str(exc))
 132 |         except Exception as exc:  # pragma: no cover - safety net
 133 |             return ToolResult(ok=False, error=f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}")
 134 | 
 135 | 
 136 | def build_default_registry(*, include_shell: bool = True) -> ToolRegistry:
 137 |     """Construct the default tool set used by the agent."""
 138 |     # Imports are local to avoid an import cycle at module load time.
 139 |     from modkit.tools.fs import build_fs_tools
 140 |     from modkit.tools.docs_tools import build_docs_tools
 141 |     from modkit.tools.mod_tools import build_mod_tools
 142 |     from modkit.tools.shell_tool import build_shell_tool
 143 | 
 144 |     registry = ToolRegistry()
 145 |     for t in build_fs_tools():
 146 |         registry.register(t)
 147 |     for t in build_docs_tools():
 148 |         registry.register(t)
 149 |     for t in build_mod_tools():
 150 |         registry.register(t)
 151 |     if include_shell:
 152 |         registry.register(build_shell_tool())
 153 |     return registry

--- modkit/tools/shell_tool.py (Python, 81 lines, ~726 tokens) ---
   1 | """Shell command tool — always gated behind permission checks."""
   2 | 
   3 | from __future__ import annotations
   4 | 
   5 | import platform
   6 | import subprocess
   7 | from typing import Any
   8 | 
   9 | from modkit.permissions import Kind
  10 | from modkit.tools.registry import Tool, ToolContext, ToolResult
  11 | 
  12 | 
  13 | def _shell(args: dict[str, Any], ctx: ToolContext) -> ToolResult:
  14 |     command = str(args.get("command") or "").strip()
  15 |     if not command:
  16 |         return ToolResult(ok=False, error="'command' is required")
  17 |     timeout = int(args.get("timeout") or 60)
  18 |     timeout = max(1, min(timeout, 600))
  19 | 
  20 |     cwd = ctx.shell_cwd or ctx.mod_root or ctx.mods_root
  21 |     try:
  22 |         if platform.system() == "Windows":
  23 |             cmd = ["powershell", "-NoProfile", "-NonInteractive", "-Command", command]
  24 |             completed = subprocess.run(
  25 |                 cmd,
  26 |                 cwd=str(cwd),
  27 |                 capture_output=True,
  28 |                 text=True,
  29 |                 timeout=timeout,
  30 |             )
  31 |         else:
  32 |             completed = subprocess.run(
  33 |                 command,
  34 |                 shell=True,
  35 |                 cwd=str(cwd),
  36 |                 capture_output=True,
  37 |                 text=True,
  38 |                 timeout=timeout,
  39 |             )
  40 |     except subprocess.TimeoutExpired as exc:
  41 |         return ToolResult(
  42 |             ok=False,
  43 |             error=f"shell command timed out after {timeout}s",
  44 |             data={"command": command, "timeout": timeout, "stderr": str(exc)},
  45 |         )
  46 | 
  47 |     stdout = (completed.stdout or "")[-20000:]
  48 |     stderr = (completed.stderr or "")[-20000:]
  49 |     return ToolResult(
  50 |         ok=completed.returncode == 0,
  51 |         data={
  52 |             "command": command,
  53 |             "returncode": completed.returncode,
  54 |             "stdout": stdout,
  55 |             "stderr": stderr,
  56 |             "cwd": str(cwd),
  57 |         },
  58 |     )
  59 | 
  60 | 
  61 | def build_shell_tool() -> Tool:
  62 |     return Tool(
  63 |         name="shell",
  64 |         description=(
  65 |             "Run a shell command in the selected mod directory (or mods root if no mod "
  66 |             "is selected). On Windows the command is executed via PowerShell. Always "
  67 |             "asks for user approval in modes ask/auto-edit; yolo mode runs it unattended."
  68 |         ),
  69 |         parameters={
  70 |             "type": "object",
  71 |             "properties": {
  72 |                 "command": {"type": "string"},
  73 |                 "timeout": {"type": "integer", "minimum": 1, "maximum": 600},
  74 |             },
  75 |             "required": ["command"],
  76 |             "additionalProperties": False,
  77 |         },
  78 |         kind=Kind.SHELL,
  79 |         handler=_shell,
  80 |     )

--- modkit/tui/__init__.py (Python, 30 lines, ~264 tokens) ---
   1 | """Textual TUI for metera-modkit.
   2 | 
   3 | The TUI is a full-screen interactive interface intended for
   4 | modders who prefer not to type ``modkit <subcommand>`` all the
   5 | time. It provides:
   6 | 
   7 | * a mod browser with create / duplicate / delete / export actions,
   8 | * a file tree of the selected mod,
   9 | * a tabbed editor with JSON syntax highlighting + live validation,
  10 | * a schema panel that shows the relevant ``runtime_manifest``
  11 |   entry for the current file,
  12 | * an AI agent chat panel (only enabled when a provider is
  13 |   configured),
  14 | * quick-create templates for the most common mod types.
  15 | 
  16 | Launch with ``modkit tui`` or just ``modkit`` (no args).
  17 | """

  24 |     TEMPLATES,
  25 |     get_template,
  26 |     write_template,
  27 | )
  28 |

   1 | """Textual app for metera-modkit.
   2 | 
   3 | Layout (5 columns split into 3 panes):
   4 | 
   5 |     ┌─ Header: modkit · <mods root> · <provider> ─────────────────────┐
   6 |     │ Mods (sidebar) │ Files (tree)  │ Editor / Schema / Validate / AI │
   7 |     │                │               │                                  │
   8 |     │                │               │                                  │
   9 |     └─ Footer: keybindings ─────────────────────────────────────────────┘
  10 | 
  11 | The app deliberately keeps state minimal: it never holds a file
  12 | buffer that is out of sync with disk. Every "save" reads from the
  13 | TextArea, every "load" pushes to it. That makes undo / redo and
  14 | external edits behave naturally.

  61 | # ── helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  62 | 
  63 | 
  64 | # tree-sitter is required for TextArea syntax highlighting. We try
  65 | # to use it if the user has the language packages installed, but
  66 | # never crash if they're missing.
  67 | _AVAILABLE_LANGUAGES: set[str] = set()
  68 | try:  # pragma: no cover - best-effort detection
  69 |     from textual.document._languages import (  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
  70 |         BUILTIN_LANGUAGES,
  71 |     )
  72 | 
  73 |     _AVAILABLE_LANGUAGES = set(BUILTIN_LANGUAGES)
  74 | except Exception:  # pragma: no cover
  75 |     _AVAILABLE_LANGUAGES = {"json", "markdown", "python", "css", "html", "javascript"}
  76 | 
  77 | 
  78 | def _pick_language(path: Path) -> str | None:
  79 |     """Return a tree-sitter language name for ``path`` or None if
  80 |     the corresponding package is not installed."""
  81 |     if not path:
  82 |         return None
  83 |     ext = path.suffix.lower()
  84 |     mapping = {
  85 |         ".json": "json",
  86 |         ".md": "markdown",
  87 |         ".py": "python",
  88 |         ".js": "javascript",
  89 |         ".css": "css",
  90 |         ".html": "html",
  91 |     }
  92 |     name = mapping.get(ext)
  93 |     if name and name in _AVAILABLE_LANGUAGES:
  94 |         return name
  95 |     return None
  96 | 
  97 | 
  98 | def _open_in_explorer(path: Path) -> None:
  99 |     """Open ``path`` in the platform file manager."""
 100 |     path = path.resolve()
 101 |     if platform.system() == "Windows":
 102 |         os.startfile(str(path))  # noqa: S606 - intentional user action
 103 |     elif platform.system() == "Darwin":
 104 |         subprocess.Popen(["open", str(path)])
 105 |     else:
 106 |         subprocess.Popen(["xdg-open", str(path)])
 107 | 
 108 | 
 109 | def _export_zip(src: Path, dest: Path) -> None:
 110 |     """Pack ``src`` directory into a .zip at ``dest``."""
 111 |     with zipfile.ZipFile(dest, "w", zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as zf:
 112 |         for root, _, files in os.walk(src):
 113 |             for f in files:
 114 |                 full = Path(root) / f
 115 |                 zf.write(full, full.relative_to(src.parent))
 116 | 
 117 | 
 118 | def _validate_id(value: str) -> str | None:
 119 |     """Return an error string if the id is not a valid mod id, else None."""
 120 |     if not value:
 121 |         return "id не может быть пустым"
 122 |     import re
 123 | 
 124 |     if not re.fullmatch(r"[a-z0-9_]+", value):
 125 |         return "id: только [a-z0-9_]+ (латиница в нижнем регистре, цифры, подчёркивания)"
 126 |     return None
 127 | 
 128 | 
 129 | def _chat_record_to_rich(record: ChatRecord) -> str:
 130 |     title = rich_escape(record.title)
 131 |     body = rich_escape(record.body)
 132 |     if record.kind == "assistant":
 133 |         return f"[bold green]Р°РіРµРЅС‚:[/bold green] {body}"
 134 |     if record.kind == "tool_call":
 135 |         return f"[yellow]{title}[/yellow] {body}".rstrip()
 136 |     if record.kind == "tool_result":
 137 |         color = "green" if record.title.startswith("ok ") else "red"
 138 |         return f"[{color}]{title}[/{color}]" + (f" {body}" if body else "")
 139 |     if record.kind == "error":
 140 |         return f"[red]{title}: {body}[/red]"
 141 |     if record.kind == "done":
 142 |         return f"[dim]{title}[/dim]"
 143 |     return f"[dim]{title}[/dim]" + (f" {body}" if body else "")

 408 |     def on_mount(self) -> None:
 409 |         table = self.query_one("#mods-table", DataTable)
 410 |         table.add_columns("id", "v", "name")
 411 |         self._refresh_mods()
 412 |         self._refresh_header()
 413 |         if not self.cfg.provider:
 414 |             self.notify(
 415 |                 "AI-провайдер не настроен. Запусти `modkit init` в обычном терминале.",
 416 |                 severity="warning",
 417 |                 timeout=10,
 418 |             )
 419 | 
 420 |     def _refresh_header(self) -> None:
 421 |         provider = self.cfg.provider or "(не настроен)"
 422 |         self.sub_title = f"v{__version__}  ·  {self._mods_root}  ·  {provider}"
 423 | 
 424 |     # ── mods table ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
 425 | 
 426 |     def _refresh_mods(self, select: str | None = None) -> None:
 427 |         table = self.query_one("#mods-table", DataTable)
 428 |         table.clear()
 429 |         if not self._mods_root.exists():
 430 |             return
 431 |         for mod_dir in sorted(self._mods_root.iterdir(), key=lambda p: p.name.lower()):
 432 |             if not mod_dir.is_dir():
 433 |                 continue
 434 |             mjson = mod_dir / "mod.json"
 435 |             version = "?"
 436 |             name = mod_dir.name
 437 |             if mjson.exists():
 438 |                 try:
 439 |                     with mjson.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
 440 |                         data = json.load(fh)
 441 |                     version = data.get("version", "?")
 442 |                     name = data.get("name") or mod_dir.name
 443 |                 except Exception:
 444 |                     pass
 445 |             table.add_row(mod_dir.name, str(version), str(name), key=mod_dir.name)
 446 |         if select:
 447 |             self._select_mod(select)
 448 | 
 449 |     def _select_mod(self, mod_id: str) -> None:
 450 |         self.current_mod = mod_id
 451 |         tree = self.query_one("#files-tree", DirectoryTree)
 452 |         mod_path = self._mods_root / mod_id
 453 |         if mod_path.exists():
 454 |             tree.path = mod_path
 455 |         else:
 456 |             tree.path = self._mods_root
 457 |         # reset editor
 458 |         self.current_file = None
 459 |         self._editor.path = None
 460 |         self._editor.dirty = False
 461 |         editor = self.query_one("#editor", TextArea)
 462 |         editor.text = ""
 463 |         editor.read_only = True
 464 |         self.query_one("#schema-body", Static).update("(выбери файл)")
 465 |         self.query_one("#validate-body", Static).update("(нажми F5)")
 466 | 
 467 |     @on(DataTable.RowHighlighted, "#mods-table")
 468 |     def _on_mod_row_highlighted(self, event: DataTable.RowHighlighted) -> None:
 469 |         if event.row_key and event.row_key.value:
 470 |             self._select_mod(str(event.row_key.value))
 471 | 
 472 |     # ── file tree ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 473 | 
 474 |     @on(DirectoryTree.FileSelected, "#files-tree")
 475 |     def _on_file_selected(self, event: DirectoryTree.FileSelected) -> None:
 476 |         path = Path(str(event.path))
 477 |         # Only files inside mods root are openable.
 478 |         try:
 479 |             rel = path.resolve().relative_to(self._mods_root.resolve())
 480 |         except ValueError:
 481 |             self.notify("файл вне mods root", severity="error")
 482 |             return
 483 |         parts = rel.parts
 484 |         if not parts:
 485 |             return
 486 |         # First segment must be the current mod.
 487 |         mod_id = self.current_mod or parts[0]
 488 |         if parts[0] != mod_id:
 489 |             self.notify(f"файл принадлежит '{parts[0]}', выбери этот мод в списке", severity="warning")
 490 |             return
 491 |         self._open_file(path)
 492 | 
 493 |     def _open_file(self, path: Path) -> None:
 494 |         self.current_file = path
 495 |         self._editor.path = path
 496 |         self._editor.dirty = False
 497 |         try:
 498 |             text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
 499 |         except UnicodeDecodeError:
 500 |             text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
 501 |         editor = self.query_one("#editor", TextArea)
 502 |         editor.language = _pick_language(path)
 503 |         editor.read_only = False
 504 |         editor.text = text
 505 |         self._refresh_schema(path)
 506 | 
 507 |     def _refresh_schema(self, path: Path) -> None:
 508 |         # Best-effort: infer the manifest key from the path (data/<key>.json)
 509 |         # and show the relevant section.
 510 |         rel = path.name
 511 |         manifest_key = None
 512 |         for key in docs_index.runtime_manifest_keys():
 513 |             if rel.startswith(key) or rel == f"{key}.json":
 514 |                 manifest_key = key
 515 |                 break
 516 |         body = self.query_one("#schema-body", Static)
 517 |         if not manifest_key:
 518 |             body.update(
 519 |                 f"[dim]Нет схемы для файла '{rel}'.\n"
 520 |                 f"Полный список ключей: `modkit docs --section runtime-manifest`.[/dim]"
 521 |             )
 522 |             return
 523 |         schema = docs_index.schema_lookup(manifest_key)
 524 |         sections = docs_index.find_section(manifest_key) or docs_index.find_section("runtime-manifest")
 525 |         parts: list[str] = []
 526 |         parts.append(f"[b]{manifest_key}[/b]  ({schema['manifest'].get('merge_policy', '?')})")
 527 |         if schema["manifest"].get("description"):
 528 |             parts.append("")
 529 |             parts.append(schema["manifest"]["description"])
 530 |         parts.append("")
 531 |         parts.append("[dim]Поля схемы (см. README):[/dim]")
 532 |         if sections:
 533 |             for s in sections[:2]:
 534 |                 text = s.body
 535 |                 if len(text) > 1200:
 536 |                     text = text[:1200] + "\n…(обрезано, см. полный README)"

 587 |     def _new_mod_done(self, result: _NewModResult | None) -> None:
 588 |         if not result:
 589 |             return
 590 |         target = self._mods_root / result.id
 591 |         if target.exists():
 592 |             self.notify(f"мод '{result.id}' уже существует", severity="error")
 593 |             return
 594 |         # Apply template.
 595 |         template_name = result.template.lower()
 596 |         fn = templates.get_template(template_name)
 597 |         if fn is None:
 598 |             self.notify(f"неизвестный шаблон '{template_name}'", severity="error")
 599 |             return
 600 |         ctx = {
 601 |             "id": result.id,
 602 |             "name": result.name,
 603 |             "author": result.author,
 604 |             "description": result.description,
 605 |         }
 606 |         files = fn(ctx)
 607 |         target.mkdir(parents=True)
 608 |         (target / "data").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
 609 |         from modkit.templates import write_template

 611 |         write_template(target, files)
 612 |         self._refresh_mods(select=result.id)

 615 |     def action_duplicate_mod(self) -> None:
 616 |         if not self.current_mod:
 617 |             self.notify("выбери мод в списке", severity="warning")
 618 |             return
 619 |         src = self._mods_root / self.current_mod
 620 |         if not src.exists():
 621 |             self.notify("мод не найден", severity="error")
 622 |             return
 623 |         new_id = f"{self.current_mod}_copy"
 624 |         i = 2
 625 |         while (self._mods_root / new_id).exists():
 626 |             new_id = f"{self.current_mod}_copy{i}"
 627 |             i += 1
 628 |         shutil.copytree(src, self._mods_root / new_id)
 629 |         # Update mod.json id field.
 630 |         mj = self._mods_root / new_id / "mod.json"
 631 |         if mj.exists():
 632 |             try:
 633 |                 data = json.loads(mj.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
 634 |                 data["id"] = new_id
 635 |                 mj.write_text(
 636 |                     json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2),
 637 |                     encoding="utf-8",
 638 |                 )
 639 |             except Exception:
 640 |                 pass
 641 |         self._refresh_mods(select=new_id)
 642 |         self.notify(f"продублировано как '{new_id}'", severity="information")

 700 |     def action_validate(self) -> None:
 701 |         if not self.current_mod:

 703 |             return

 712 |             lines.append(f"[red]x {e}[/red]")
 713 |         for w in report.warnings:
 714 |             lines.append(f"[yellow]! {w}[/yellow]")

 716 |         self.last_validation = body

 719 |         tabs = self.query_one("TabbedContent")

 762 |     def _send_chat(self) -> None:

 774 |     @work(exclusive=True, thread=True)
 775 |     def _run_agent(self, task: str) -> None:
 776 |         from modkit.agent import run_agent
 777 |         from modkit.permissions import Mode
 778 |         from modkit.providers.base import ProviderError
 779 |         from modkit.tools import build_default_registry
 780 |         from modkit.tools.registry import ToolContext
 781 | 
 782 |         log = self.query_one("#chat-log", RichLog)
 783 |         try:
 784 |             provider = build_provider(
 785 |                 provider_id=self.cfg.provider,
 786 |                 api_key=self.cfg.api_key_for(self.cfg.provider) or "",
 787 |                 model=self.cfg.model or "",
 788 |                 base_url=self.cfg.base_url or "",
 789 |                 temperature=self.cfg.temperature,
 790 |                 max_tokens=self.cfg.max_tokens,
 791 |             )
 792 |         except ProviderError as exc:
 793 |             self.call_from_thread(log.write, f"[red]provider error: {exc}[/red]")
 794 |             return
 795 | 
 796 |         mods_root = self._mods_root
 797 |         mod_root = (mods_root / self.current_mod) if self.current_mod else None
 798 |         ctx = ToolContext(
 799 |             mods_root=mods_root,
 800 |             mod_root=mod_root,
 801 |             mode=Mode(self.cfg.permission_mode),
 802 |             confirm=lambda name, args: True,  # UI handles prompting via Dialog
 803 |         )
 804 |         registry = build_default_registry(include_shell=bool(self.cfg.provider))
 805 | 
 806 |         def on_event(event) -> None:
 807 |             record = event_to_record(event)
 808 |             self.call_from_thread(log.write, _chat_record_to_rich(record))
 809 | 
 810 |         try:
 811 |             final = run_agent(
 812 |                 provider=provider,
 813 |                 registry=registry,
 814 |                 ctx=ctx,
 815 |                 user_task=task,
 816 |                 history=self.chat_history,
 817 |                 max_iterations=self.cfg.max_iterations or 12,
 818 |                 on_event=on_event,
 819 |             )
 820 |         except ProviderError as exc:
 821 |             self.call_from_thread(log.write, f"[red]error: {exc}[/red]")
 822 |             return
 823 |             
 824 |         self.chat_history = final
 825 |         # Refresh mod list in case the agent created something.
 826 |         self.call_from_thread(self._refresh_mods, self.current_mod)

--- modkit/ui.py (Python, 167 lines, ~1,152 tokens) ---
   1 | """Terminal output helpers: colored printing, prompts, progress, JSON dump."""
   2 | 
   3 | from __future__ import annotations
   4 | 
   5 | import json
   6 | import os
   7 | import sys
   8 | from typing import Any
   9 | 
  10 | 
  11 | def _is_tty(stream) -> bool:
  12 |     """Tolerant isatty() that handles GUI-subsystem .exe where streams are None."""
  13 |     if stream is None:
  14 |         return False
  15 |     isatty = getattr(stream, "isatty", None)
  16 |     if not callable(isatty):
  17 |         return False
  18 |     try:
  19 |         return bool(isatty())
  20 |     except Exception:
  21 |         return False
  22 | 
  23 | 
  24 | def _safe_stream(name: str):
  25 |     """Return sys.<name> or a dummy stream so print()/json.dump() never crash
  26 |     when the .exe is built with console=False (Windows GUI subsystem)."""
  27 |     stream = getattr(sys, name, None)
  28 |     if stream is not None:
  29 |         return stream
  30 | 
  31 |     class _Dummy:
  32 |         def write(self, *_args, **_kwargs):
  33 |             return 0
  34 | 
  35 |         def flush(self):
  36 |             return None
  37 | 
  38 |         def isatty(self):
  39 |             return False
  40 | 
  41 |     return _Dummy()
  42 | 
  43 | 
  44 | _USE_COLOR_DEFAULT = (
  45 |     _is_tty(sys.stdout)
  46 |     and os.environ.get("NO_COLOR") is None
  47 |     and os.environ.get("TERM") != "dumb"
  48 | )
  49 | 
  50 | 
  51 | class _Style:
  52 |     reset = "\x1b[0m"
  53 |     dim = "\x1b[2m"
  54 |     bold = "\x1b[1m"
  55 |     red = "\x1b[31m"
  56 |     green = "\x1b[32m"
  57 |     yellow = "\x1b[33m"
  58 |     blue = "\x1b[34m"
  59 |     magenta = "\x1b[35m"
  60 |     cyan = "\x1b[36m"
  61 |     gray = "\x1b[90m"
  62 | 
  63 | 
  64 | _color_enabled = _USE_COLOR_DEFAULT
  65 | 
  66 | 
  67 | def set_color(enabled: bool) -> None:
  68 |     global _color_enabled
  69 |     _color_enabled = bool(enabled)
  70 | 
  71 | 
  72 | def _wrap(code: str, text: str) -> str:
  73 |     if not _color_enabled:
  74 |         return text
  75 |     return f"{code}{text}{_Style.reset}"
  76 | 
  77 | 
  78 | def info(msg: str) -> None:
  79 |     print(_wrap(_Style.cyan, "i ") + msg)
  80 | 
  81 | 
  82 | def success(msg: str) -> None:
  83 |     print(_wrap(_Style.green, "+ ") + msg)
  84 | 
  85 | 
  86 | def warn(msg: str) -> None:
  87 |     print(_wrap(_Style.yellow, "! ") + msg)
  88 | 
  89 | 
  90 | def error(msg: str) -> None:
  91 |     print(_wrap(_Style.red, "x ") + msg, file=_safe_stream("stderr"))
  92 | 
  93 | 
  94 | def hint(msg: str) -> None:
  95 |     print(_wrap(_Style.gray, msg))
  96 | 
  97 | 
  98 | def header(msg: str) -> None:
  99 |     print(_wrap(_Style.bold, msg))
 100 | 
 101 | 
 102 | def dim(text: str) -> str:
 103 |     return _wrap(_Style.dim, text)
 104 | 
 105 | 
 106 | def bold(text: str) -> str:
 107 |     return _wrap(_Style.bold, text)
 108 | 
 109 | 
 110 | def color(name: str, text: str) -> str:
 111 |     code = getattr(_Style, name, "")
 112 |     if not code:
 113 |         return text
 114 |     return _wrap(code, text)
 115 | 
 116 | 
 117 | def confirm(question: str, default: bool = False) -> bool:
 118 |     """Ask user a yes/no question. Returns True for yes."""
 119 |     suffix = " [Y/n] " if default else " [y/N] "
 120 |     while True:
 121 |         try:
 122 |             raw = input(_wrap(_Style.yellow, "? ") + question + suffix).strip().lower()
 123 |         except EOFError:
 124 |             return default
 125 |         if not raw:
 126 |             return default
 127 |         if raw in ("y", "yes", "д", "да"):
 128 |             return True
 129 |         if raw in ("n", "no", "н", "нет"):
 130 |             return False
 131 | 
 132 | 
 133 | def choose(question: str, options: list[str], default: int = 0) -> int:
 134 |     """Numbered menu. Returns the chosen index."""
 135 |     print(_wrap(_Style.yellow, "? ") + question)
 136 |     for idx, opt in enumerate(options, 1):
 137 |         marker = "*" if idx - 1 == default else " "
 138 |         print(f"  {marker} {idx}. {opt}")
 139 |     while True:
 140 |         try:
 141 |             raw = input(f"Выбор [1-{len(options)}, по умолчанию {default + 1}]: ").strip()
 142 |         except EOFError:
 143 |             return default
 144 |         if not raw:
 145 |             return default
 146 |         if raw.isdigit():
 147 |             num = int(raw)
 148 |             if 1 <= num <= len(options):
 149 |                 return num - 1
 150 |         print(_wrap(_Style.red, "Неверный выбор."))
 151 | 
 152 | 
 153 | def ask(prompt: str, default: str = "") -> str:
 154 |     suffix = f" [{default}]" if default else ""
 155 |     try:
 156 |         raw = input(_wrap(_Style.yellow, "? ") + prompt + suffix + ": ").strip()
 157 |     except EOFError:
 158 |         return default
 159 |     return raw or default
 160 | 
 161 | 
 162 | def write_json(payload: Any, *, indent: int = 2) -> None:
 163 |     out = _safe_stream("stdout")
 164 |     json.dump(payload, out, ensure_ascii=False, indent=indent)
 165 |     out.write("\n")
 166 |     out.flush()

--- modkit/validate.py (Python, 229 lines, ~2,308 tokens) ---
   1 | """Mod validation against the Chronicles of Meterea modding contract.
   2 | 
   3 | This is the pure-logic version of what the GUI ModKit did inside
   4 | ``_collect_mod_validation``. We use it both as a CLI subcommand and as
   5 | an agent tool.
   6 | """
   7 | 
   8 | from __future__ import annotations
   9 | 
  10 | import json
  11 | import re
  12 | from dataclasses import dataclass, field
  13 | from pathlib import Path
  14 | from typing import Any
  15 | 
  16 | from modkit.docs import runtime_manifest
  17 | 
  18 | 
  19 | MOD_ID_RE = re.compile(r"^[a-z0-9_]+$")
  20 | SEMVER_RE = re.compile(r"^\d+\.\d+\.\d+(?:-[\w.]+)?(?:\+[\w.]+)?$")
  21 | 
  22 | 
  23 | @dataclass
  24 | class ValidationReport:
  25 |     mod_id: str
  26 |     mod_path: str
  27 |     errors: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
  28 |     warnings: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
  29 |     info: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
  30 | 
  31 |     @property
  32 |     def ok(self) -> bool:
  33 |         return not self.errors
  34 | 
  35 |     def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
  36 |         return {
  37 |             "mod_id": self.mod_id,
  38 |             "mod_path": self.mod_path,
  39 |             "ok": self.ok,
  40 |             "errors": list(self.errors),
  41 |             "warnings": list(self.warnings),
  42 |             "info": list(self.info),
  43 |         }
  44 | 
  45 | 
  46 | def validate_mod(mod_path: Path) -> ValidationReport:
  47 |     """Run the full set of static checks on a mod folder."""
  48 |     mod_path = Path(mod_path).resolve()
  49 |     report = ValidationReport(mod_id=mod_path.name, mod_path=str(mod_path))
  50 | 
  51 |     if not mod_path.exists() or not mod_path.is_dir():
  52 |         report.errors.append(f"Mod folder not found: {mod_path}")
  53 |         return report
  54 | 
  55 |     mod_json_path = mod_path / "mod.json"
  56 |     if not mod_json_path.exists():
  57 |         report.errors.append("mod.json not found")
  58 |         return report
  59 | 
  60 |     try:
  61 |         meta_raw = mod_json_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
  62 |     except UnicodeDecodeError:
  63 |         try:
  64 |             meta_raw = mod_json_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig")
  65 |             report.warnings.append(
  66 |                 "mod.json saved with a UTF-8 BOM — strip it before shipping the mod"
  67 |             )
  68 |         except OSError as exc:
  69 |             report.errors.append(f"Cannot read mod.json: {exc}")
  70 |             return report
  71 |     except OSError as exc:
  72 |         report.errors.append(f"Cannot read mod.json: {exc}")
  73 |         return report
  74 |     # Some editors (and PowerShell's Set-Content -Encoding utf8) prepend a BOM
  75 |     # even when the file decodes as UTF-8. Strip it once before json.loads.
  76 |     if meta_raw.startswith("\ufeff"):
  77 |         meta_raw = meta_raw.lstrip("\ufeff")
  78 |         report.warnings.append(
  79 |             "mod.json saved with a UTF-8 BOM — strip it before shipping the mod"
  80 |         )
  81 |     try:
  82 |         meta: dict[str, Any] = json.loads(meta_raw)
  83 |     except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
  84 |         report.errors.append(f"mod.json is not valid JSON: {exc}")
  85 |         return report
  86 | 
  87 |     _validate_descriptor(meta, mod_path.name, report)
  88 |     _validate_files(meta, mod_path, report)
  89 |     _validate_data_section(meta, mod_path, report)
  90 |     _validate_total_conversion(meta, report)
  91 | 
  92 |     return report
  93 | 
  94 | 
  95 | def _validate_descriptor(meta: dict[str, Any], folder_name: str, report: ValidationReport) -> None:
  96 |     required = ["id", "name", "version"]
  97 |     recommended = ["author", "description"]
  98 | 
  99 |     for field_name in required:
 100 |         value = meta.get(field_name)
 101 |         if not isinstance(value, str) or not value.strip():
 102 |             report.errors.append(f"mod.json missing required string field '{field_name}'")
 103 | 
 104 |     for field_name in recommended:
 105 |         value = meta.get(field_name)
 106 |         if not isinstance(value, str) or not value.strip():
 107 |             report.warnings.append(f"mod.json missing recommended field '{field_name}'")
 108 | 
 109 |     mod_id = meta.get("id")
 110 |     if isinstance(mod_id, str):
 111 |         if not MOD_ID_RE.match(mod_id):
 112 |             report.errors.append(
 113 |                 "mod.json 'id' must match [a-z0-9_]+ (lowercase ASCII letters, digits, underscores)"
 114 |             )
 115 |         if mod_id != folder_name:
 116 |             report.warnings.append(
 117 |                 f"mod.json id '{mod_id}' does not match folder name '{folder_name}'"
 118 |             )
 119 | 
 120 |     version = meta.get("version")
 121 |     if isinstance(version, str) and version and not SEMVER_RE.match(version):
 122 |         report.warnings.append(
 123 |             f"mod.json 'version' = '{version}' is not semver (expected like 1.0.0)"
 124 |         )
 125 | 
 126 |     deps = meta.get("dependencies")
 127 |     if deps is not None and not isinstance(deps, list):
 128 |         report.errors.append("mod.json 'dependencies' must be an array of strings")
 129 |     elif isinstance(deps, list):
 130 |         for dep in deps:
 131 |             if not isinstance(dep, str):
 132 |                 report.errors.append(
 133 |                     f"mod.json 'dependencies' contains non-string value: {dep!r}"
 134 |                 )
 135 | 
 136 |     tc = meta.get("total_conversion")
 137 |     if tc is not None and not isinstance(tc, bool):
 138 |         report.errors.append("mod.json 'total_conversion' must be boolean")
 139 | 
 140 | 
 141 | def _validate_files(meta: dict[str, Any], mod_path: Path, report: ValidationReport) -> None:
 142 |     scripts = meta.get("scripts")
 143 |     if scripts is None:
 144 |         return
 145 |     if not isinstance(scripts, list):
 146 |         report.errors.append("mod.json 'scripts' must be an array of strings")
 147 |         return
 148 |     for script in scripts:
 149 |         if not isinstance(script, str):
 150 |             report.errors.append(f"mod.json 'scripts' contains non-string: {script!r}")
 151 |             continue
 152 |         script_path = mod_path / script
 153 |         if not script_path.exists():
 154 |             report.errors.append(f"script not found: {script}")
 155 | 
 156 | 
 157 | def _validate_data_section(meta: dict[str, Any], mod_path: Path, report: ValidationReport) -> None:
 158 |     data = meta.get("data")
 159 |     if data is None:
 160 |         return
 161 |     if not isinstance(data, dict):
 162 |         report.errors.append("mod.json 'data' must be an object")
 163 |         return
 164 | 
 165 |     manifest = runtime_manifest()
 166 |     known_keys = set(manifest.get("database_files", {}).keys())
 167 | 
 168 |     for key, entries in data.items():
 169 |         if known_keys and key not in known_keys:
 170 |             report.warnings.append(
 171 |                 f"data key '{key}' is not in runtime_manifest.json — typo?"
 172 |             )
 173 |         if isinstance(entries, str):
 174 |             entries_iter = [entries]
 175 |         elif isinstance(entries, list):
 176 |             entries_iter = entries
 177 |         else:
 178 |             report.errors.append(
 179 |                 f"data['{key}'] must be a string or array of strings"
 180 |             )
 181 |             continue
 182 |         for rel in entries_iter:
 183 |             if not isinstance(rel, str):
 184 |                 report.errors.append(
 185 |                     f"data['{key}'] entry is not a string: {rel!r}"
 186 |                 )
 187 |                 continue
 188 |             path = mod_path / rel
 189 |             if not path.exists():
 190 |                 report.errors.append(f"data file referenced by '{key}' not found: {rel}")
 191 |             else:
 192 |                 if path.suffix.lower() == ".json":
 193 |                     try:
 194 |                         json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
 195 |                     except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
 196 |                         report.errors.append(f"{rel} is not valid JSON: {exc}")
 197 | 
 198 | 
 199 | def _validate_total_conversion(meta: dict[str, Any], report: ValidationReport) -> None:
 200 |     if not meta.get("total_conversion"):
 201 |         return
 202 |     manifest = runtime_manifest()
 203 |     required: list[str] = (
 204 |         manifest.get("modding_contract", {})
 205 |         .get("total_conversion", {})
 206 |         .get("required_database_keys", [])
 207 |     )
 208 |     data = meta.get("data") or {}
 209 |     for key in required:
 210 |         entries = data.get(key)
 211 |         if not entries:
 212 |             report.errors.append(
 213 |                 f"total_conversion=true but data['{key}'] is missing (required for TC mods)"
 214 |             )
 215 | 
 216 | 
 217 | def validate_json_file(path: Path) -> tuple[bool, str]:
 218 |     """Return (ok, message) for a single JSON file."""
 219 |     path = Path(path)
 220 |     if not path.exists():
 221 |         return False, f"file not found: {path}"
 222 |     try:
 223 |         json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
 224 |     except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
 225 |         return False, f"invalid JSON: {exc}"
 226 |     except OSError as exc:
 227 |         return False, f"cannot read: {exc}"
 228 |     return True, "JSON is valid"
